<content>
1: // Package pipeline is the minimum integration layer for Wave 4.
2: //
3: // It chains normalize → derive → belief.Process → ProposeIfNew → intent.Audit
4: // into a single pass over evidence fixtures. The kernel owns correctness;
5: // this package is a thin orchestrator.
6: //
7: // Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution;
8: // concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.
9: package pipeline
10: 
11: import (
12: 	"context"
13: 	"database/sql"
14: 	"errors"
15: 	"fmt"
16: 	"log/slog"
17: 	"os"
18: 	"path/filepath"
19: 	"regexp"
20: 	"sort"
21: 
22: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/belief"
23: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/derive"
24: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/intent"
25: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/kernel"
26: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/normalize"
27: )
28: 
29: // Result captures the outcome of processing one evidence item through the pipeline.
30: type Result struct {
31: 	Normalized    normalize.NormalizedEvidence
32: 	Beliefs       []derive.DerivedBelief
33: 	BeliefID      string // empty if contradiction or no belief emitted
34: 	Promoted      bool
35: 	DebtItems     []string // remaining debt after processing
36: 	IntentState   string   // "live", "cancelled", or "" (no intent proposed)
37: 	Contradiction bool     // true if this result is a contradiction (no ledger mutation)
38: }
39: 
40: // sourceTypeMap maps fixture filenames to their source types.
41: // The filename-to-source-type mapping is deterministic and compile-time.
42: var sourceTypeMap = map[string]string{
43: 	"kev_entry.json":                normalize.SourceKEVEntry,
44: 	"release_with_fix.json":         normalize.SourceRelease,
45: 	"maintainer_fixes.json":         normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
46: 	"maintainer_no_regression.json": normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
47: 	"maintainer_reviewed.json":      normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
48: 	"release_confirmed.json":        normalize.SourceRelease,
49: 	"contradicting_evidence.json":   normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
50: 	"non_maintainer_comment.json":   normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
51: 	"unmerged_pr.json":              normalize.SourceGitHubPR,
52: }
53: 
54: // ProcessEvidence chains normalize → derive → belief.Process for one raw evidence item.
55: //
56: // Normal path:
57: //
58: //	normalize → derive → belief.Process (EnsureBelief, AddEvidence, RetireDebt, Promote)
59: //	→ Result with BeliefID set, Contradiction=false
60: //
61: // Contradiction path (DerivedBelief.Contradicts non-empty):
62: //
63: //	→ Result with BeliefID="", Contradiction=true
64: //	→ slog.Warn emitted with source_url
65: //	→ NO ledger mutation (belief.Process returns nil without DB writes)
66: func ProcessEvidence(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string, raw []byte, sourceType string) ([]Result, error) {
67: 	norm, err := normalize.Normalize(raw, sourceType)
68: 	if err != nil {
69: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: normalize: %w", err)
70: 	}
71: 
72: 	derived := derive.Derive(norm)
73: 	if len(derived) == 0 {
74: 		return nil, nil
75: 	}
76: 
77: 	var results []Result
78: 	for _, d := range derived {
79: 		r := Result{
80: 			Normalized: norm,
81: 			Beliefs:    []derive.DerivedBelief{d},
82: 		}
83: 
84: 		// Contradiction path: no ledger mutation.
85: 		if len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {
86: 			r.Contradiction = true
87: 			for _, c := range d.Contradicts {
88: 				slog.Warn("pipeline: contradiction received, no ledger mutation",
89: 					"source_url", c.SourceURL,
90: 					"source_type", c.SourceType,
91: 					"claim", d.Claim,
92: 				)
93: 			}
94: 			results = append(results, r)
95: 			continue
96: 		}
97: 
98: 		// Normal path: belief.Process → kernel operations.
99: 		if err := belief.Process(ctx, db, scenarioID, d); err != nil {
100: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: belief.Process: %w", err)
101: 		}
102: 
103: 		// Query the belief ID and state.
104: 		beliefID, status, debtLen, err := queryBelief(ctx, db, scenarioID, d.Claim)
105: 		if err != nil {
106: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: query belief: %w", err)
107: 		}
108: 
109: 		r.BeliefID = beliefID
110: 		r.Promoted = status == "promoted"
111: 		r.DebtItems = make([]string, debtLen)
112: 		results = append(results, r)
113: 	}
114: 
115: 	return results, nil
116: }
117: 
118: // aggregateDeriveResults collects all fixture derive outputs and merges supporting
119: // evidence from derived beliefs that reference the same vulnerability into a single
120: // DerivedBelief. The first CVE-bearing claim becomes the primary claim; all other
121: // non-contradiction evidence is merged into it as supporting evidence.
122: // Contradictions are collected separately. Returns the merged beliefs and contradictions.
123: func aggregateDeriveResults(allFixtureResults [][]derive.DerivedBelief) ([]derive.DerivedBelief, []derive.DerivedBelief) {
124: 	cvePattern := regexp.MustCompile(`CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}`)
125: 
126: 	// Collect all claims and their evidence.
127: 	type claimEntry struct {
128: 		classification string
129: 		evidence       []normalize.NormalizedEvidence
130: 	}
131: 	claims := make(map[string]*claimEntry)
132: 	var contradictions []derive.DerivedBelief
133: 
134: 	// Find the primary vulnerability claim (first CVE-bearing claim).
135: 	var primaryClaim string
136: 
137: 	for _, fixtureResults := range allFixtureResults {
138: 		for _, d := range fixtureResults {
139: 			if len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {
140: 				contradictions = append(contradictions, d)
141: 				continue
142: 			}
143: 			if d.Claim == "" {
144: 				continue
145: 			}
146: 
147: 			// If this claim has a CVE and we haven't set a primary, set it.
148: 			if cvePattern.MatchString(d.Claim) && primaryClaim == "" {
149: 				primaryClaim = d.Claim
150: 			}
151: 
152: 			// If we have a primary claim, merge ALL other evidence into it.
153: 			// This consolidates release, maintainer, and other supporting evidence
154: 			// under the original vulnerability claim.
155: 			if primaryClaim != "" && d.Claim != primaryClaim {
156: 				if entry, ok := claims[primaryClaim]; ok {
157: 					entry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
158: 				} else {
159: 					claims[primaryClaim] = &claimEntry{
160: 						classification: d.Classification,
161: 						evidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,
162: 					}
163: 				}
164: 				continue
165: 			}
166: 
167: 			// Primary claim or no primary set yet: store normally.
168: 			if entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {
169: 				entry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
170: 			} else {
171: 				claims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{
172: 					classification: d.Classification,
173: 					evidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,
174: 				}
175: 			}
176: 		}
177: 	}
178: 
179: 	var merged []derive.DerivedBelief
180: 	for claim, entry := range claims {
181: 		merged = append(merged, derive.DerivedBelief{
182: 			Claim:              claim,
183: 			Classification:     entry.classification,
184: 			SupportingEvidence: entry.evidence,
185: 		})
186: 	}
187: 
188: 	return merged, contradictions
189: }
190: 
191: // ProposeIfNew records a live action intent on a promoted belief only if no live intent
192: // already exists for the same belief/action pair. Idempotent: replaying the same evidence
193: // does not create duplicate intents.
194: //
195: // Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution;
196: // concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.
197: func ProposeIfNew(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID, beliefID, action string) error {
198: 	var count int
199: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
200: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM action_intent
201: 		 WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID AND belief_id=$2::UUID AND action=$3::STRING AND state='live'`,
202: 		scenarioID, beliefID, action).Scan(&count)
203: 	if err != nil {
204: 		return fmt.Errorf("pipeline: check existing intent: %w", err)
205: 	}
206: 	if count > 0 {
207: 		return nil
208: 	}
209: 	return intent.Propose(ctx, db, scenarioID, beliefID, action)
210: }
211: 
212: // Run processes a directory of evidence fixtures in deterministic order.
213: // Returns results in processing order.
214: func Run(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string, fixtureDir string) ([]Result, error) {
215: 	entries, err := os.ReadDir(fixtureDir)
216: 	if err != nil {
217: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: read fixture dir: %w", err)
218: 	}
219: 
220: 	// Deterministic order: sort by filename.
221: 	var filenames []string
222: 	for _, e := range entries {
223: 		if e.IsDir() {
224: 			continue
225: 		}
226: 		if filepath.Ext(e.Name()) != ".json" {
227: 			continue
228: 		}
229: 		filenames = append(filenames, e.Name())
230: 	}
231: 	sort.Strings(filenames)
232: 
233: 	// Phase 1: derive all fixture evidence (no DB writes).
234: 	var allFixtureResults [][]derive.DerivedBelief
235: 	for _, name := range filenames {
236: 		raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(fixtureDir, name))
237: 		if err != nil {
238: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: read fixture %s: %w", name, err)
239: 		}
240: 
241: 		sourceType, ok := sourceTypeMap[name]
242: 		if !ok {
243: 			continue
244: 		}
245: 
246: 		norm, err := normalize.Normalize(raw, sourceType)
247: 		if err != nil {
248: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: normalize %s: %w", name, err)
249: 		}
250: 
251: 		allFixtureResults = append(allFixtureResults, derive.Derive(norm))
252: 	}
253: 
254: 	// Phase 2: aggregate evidence by claim.
255: 	merged, contradictions := aggregateDeriveResults(allFixtureResults)
256: 
257: 	// Phase 3: process aggregated beliefs through the ledger.
258: 	var allResults []Result
259: 
260: 	for _, d := range merged {
261: 		if err := belief.Process(ctx, db, scenarioID, d); err != nil {
262: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: belief.Process: %w", err)
263: 		}
264: 
265: 		beliefID, status, debtLen, err := queryBelief(ctx, db, scenarioID, d.Claim)
266: 		if err != nil {
267: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: query belief: %w", err)
268: 		}
269: 
270: 		allResults = append(allResults, Result{
271: 			BeliefID:  beliefID,
272: 			Promoted:  status == "promoted",
273: 			DebtItems: make([]string, debtLen),
274: 			Beliefs:   []derive.DerivedBelief{d},
275: 		})
276: 	}
277: 
278: 	// Phase 3.5: propose intents for promoted beliefs.
279: 	for i := range allResults {
280: 		r := &allResults[i]
281: 		if r.Promoted && r.BeliefID != "" {
282: 			if err := ProposeIfNew(ctx, db, scenarioID, r.BeliefID, "upgrade"); err != nil {
283: 				return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: propose intent: %w", err)
284: 			}
285: 			r.IntentState = "live"
286: 		}
287: 	}
288: 
289: 	// Phase 4: record contradictions (no ledger mutation).
290: 	for _, c := range contradictions {
291: 		allResults = append(allResults, Result{
292: 			Contradiction: true,
293: 			Beliefs:       []derive.DerivedBelief{c},
294: 		})
295: 	}
296: 
297: 	return allResults, nil
298: }
299: 
300: // queryBelief returns the belief ID, status, and remaining debt count for a claim.
301: func queryBelief(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID, claim string) (id, status string, debtLen int, err error) {
302: 	err = db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
303: 		`SELECT id, status, coalesce(array_length(debt,1),0)
304: 		 FROM belief WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID AND claim=$2`,
305: 		scenarioID, claim).Scan(&id, &status, &debtLen)
306: 	if err != nil {
307: 		return "", "", 0, err
308: 	}
309: 	return id, status, debtLen, nil
310: }
311: 
312: // AuditIntent returns the audit count for the scenario.
313: func AuditIntent(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
314: 	return intent.Audit(ctx, db, scenarioID)
315: }
316: 
317: // QueryBeliefCount returns the number of beliefs in the scenario.
318: func QueryBeliefCount(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
319: 	var count int
320: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
321: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM belief WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID`, scenarioID).Scan(&count)
322: 	return count, err
323: }
324: 
325: // QueryEvidenceCount returns the number of evidence rows in the scenario.
326: func QueryEvidenceCount(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
327: 	var count int
328: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
329: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM evidence WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID`, scenarioID).Scan(&count)
330: 	return count, err
331: }
332: 
333: // QueryIntentCount returns the number of live intents in the scenario.
334: func QueryIntentCount(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
335: 	var count int
336: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
337: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM action_intent WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID AND state='live'`, scenarioID).Scan(&count)
338: 	return count, err
339: }
340: 
341: // IsActionOnUnpromoted reports whether err is ErrActionOnUnpromoted.
342: func IsActionOnUnpromoted(err error) bool {
343: 	return errors.Is(err, kernel.ErrActionOnUnpromoted)
344: }

(End of file - total 344 lines)
</content>

138: 	if intentCount != 1 {
139: 		t.Errorf("expected 1 live intent, got %d", intentCount)
140: 	}
141: 
142: 	// Record for idempotency check.
143: 	savedBeliefCount := beliefCount
144: 	savedStatus := status
145: 	savedDebtLen := debtLen
146: 	savedEvidenceCount := evidenceCount
147: 	savedAuditCount := auditCount
148: 	savedIntentCount := intentCount
149: 
150: 	// Replay.
151: 	results2, err := pipeline.Run(ctx, shared, sc, deriveTestdataDir())
152: 	if err != nil {
153: 		t.Fatalf("Run (replay): %v", err)
154: 	}
155: 	if len(results2) != len(results) {
156: 		t.Errorf("replay produced %d results, original had %d", len(results2), len(results))
157: 	}
158: 
159: 	beliefCount2, _ := pipeline.QueryBeliefCount(ctx, shared, sc)
160: 	_, status2, debtLen2, _ := queryBeliefState(ctx, shared, sc)
161: 	evidenceCount2, _ := pipeline.QueryEvidenceCount(ctx, shared, sc)
162: 	auditCount2, _ := pipeline.AuditIntent(ctx, shared, sc)
163: 	intentCount2, _ := pipeline.QueryIntentCount(ctx, shared, sc)
164: 
165: 	if beliefCount2 != savedBeliefCount {
166: 		t.Errorf("replay belief count: expected %d, got %d", savedBeliefCount, beliefCount2)
167: 	}
168: 	if status2 != savedStatus {
169: 		t.Errorf("replay status: expected %q, got %q", savedStatus, status2)
170: 	}
171: 	if debtLen2 != savedDebtLen {
172: 		t.Errorf("replay debt len: expected %d, got %d", savedDebtLen, debtLen2)
173: 	}
174: 	if evidenceCount2 != savedEvidenceCount {
175: 		t.Errorf("replay evidence count: expected %d, got %d", savedEvidenceCount, evidenceCount2)
176: 	}
177: 	if auditCount2 != savedAuditCount {
178: 		t.Errorf("replay audit count: expected %d, got %d", savedAuditCount, auditCount2)
179: 	}
180: 	if intentCount2 != savedIntentCount {
181: 		t.Errorf("replay intent count: expected %d, got %d", savedIntentCount, intentCount2)
182: 	}
183: }

185: func TestEndToEnd_PartialDebtBlocksPromotion(t *testing.T) {
186: 	ctx := context.Background()
187: 	sc := "55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000002"
188: 
189: 	raw, err := os.ReadFile(deriveTestdataDir() + "/kev_entry.json")
190: 	if err != nil {
191: 		t.Fatalf("read fixture: %v", err)
192: 	}
193: 
194: 	results, err := pipeline.ProcessEvidence(ctx, shared, sc, raw, "kev_entry")
195: 	if err != nil {

197: 	}
198: 	if len(results) != 1 {
199: 		t.Fatalf("expected 1 result, got %d", len(results))
200: 	}
201: 	if results[0].Promoted {
202: 		t.Error("expected not promoted (partial debt), got promoted")
203: 	}

206: 	_, status, debtLen, err := queryBeliefState(ctx, shared, sc)
207: 	if err != nil {
208: 		t.Fatalf("queryBeliefState: %v", err)
209: 	}

211: 		t.Errorf("expected status 'entered', got %q", status)
212: 	}
213: 	if debtLen == 0 {
214: 		t.Error("expected non-zero debt items, got 0")
215: 	}
216: 
217: 	// Intent on unpromoted should fail.
218: 	beliefID := results[0].BeliefID
219: 	err = pipeline.ProposeIfNew(ctx, shared, sc, beliefID, "upgrade")
220: 	if err == nil {
221: 		t.Fatal("expected error for intent on unpromoted belief, got nil")
222: 	}
223: 	if !pipeline.IsActionOnUnpromoted(err) {

227: 	auditCount, err := pipeline.AuditIntent(ctx, shared, sc)
228: 	if err != nil {

230: 	}
231: 	if auditCount != 0 {
232: 		t.Errorf("expected audit=0, got %d", auditCount)
233: 	}
234: }

285: func TestEndToEnd_DeterministicResults(t *testing.T) {
286: 	ctx := context.Background()
287: 
288: 	scA := "55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000004"
289: 	scB := "55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000005"
290: 
291: 	resultsA, err := pipeline.Run(ctx, shared, scA, deriveTestdataDir())
292: 	if err != nil {
293: 		t.Fatalf("Run A: %v", err)
294: 	}
295: 
296: 	resultsB, err := pipeline.Run(ctx, shared, scB, deriveTestdataDir())
297: 	if err != nil {
298: 		t.Fatalf("Run B: %v", err)
299: 	}
300: 
301: 	if len(resultsA) != len(resultsB) {
302: 		t.Fatalf("result count mismatch: A=%d B=%d", len(resultsA), len(resultsB))
303: 	}
304: 
305: 	for i := range resultsA {
306: 		a, b := resultsA[i], resultsB[i]
307: 
308: 		// Compare deterministic fields only.
309: 		if a.Contradiction != b.Contradiction {
310: 			t.Errorf("result[%d]: Contradiction mismatch: A=%v B=%v", i, a.Contradiction, b.Contradiction)
311: 		}
312: 		if a.Contradiction {
313: 			continue // contradiction results have no belief to compare
314: 		}
315: 
316: 		if a.BeliefID == "" && b.BeliefID == "" {
317: 			continue // both empty (no belief emitted)
318: 		}
319: 		if (a.BeliefID == "") != (b.BeliefID == "") {
320: 			t.Errorf("result[%d]: BeliefID presence mismatch", i)
321: 		}
322: 	}
323: 
324: 	// Compare database state.
325: 	assertDeterministicState(t, ctx, scA, scB)
326: }

374: func TestEndToEnd_ProposeIfNew_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
375: 	ctx := context.Background()

383: 	}

386: 			t.Fatalf("RetireDebt(%s): %v", item, err)
387: 		}

402:

405: 		t.Fatalf("ProposeIfNew (2nd): %v", err)
406: 	}

409: 	if count2 != 1 {

411: 	}
412: 
413: 	// Third propose — still idempotent.
414: 	if err := pipeline.ProposeIfNew(ctx, shared, sc, beliefID, "upgrade"); err != nil {
415: 		t.Fatalf("ProposeIfNew (3rd): %v", err)
416: 	}
417: 
418: 	count3, _ := pipeline.QueryIntentCount(ctx, shared, sc)
419: 	if count3 != 1 {

424: func queryBeliefState(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (id, status string, debtLen int, err error) {
425: 	err = db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
426: 		`SELECT id, status, coalesce(array_length(debt,1),0)
427: 		 FROM belief WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID`, scenarioID).Scan(&id, &status, &debtLen)
428: 	if err != nil {
429: 		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
430: 			return "", "", 0, nil
431: 		}
432: 		return "", "", 0, err
433: 	}
434: 	return id, status, debtLen, nil
435: }

1: // Command solvent is the minimum integration layer for Wave 4.
2: //
3: // It processes evidence fixtures through the full pipeline:
4: //
5: //	normalize → derive → belief.Process → ProposeIfNew → intent.Audit
6: //
7: // and prints a deterministic transcript proving pipeline correctness.
8: //
9: // Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution;
10: // concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.
11: package main
12: 
13: import (
14: 	"context"
15: 	"flag"
16: 	"fmt"
17: 	"os"
18: 	"path/filepath"
19: 	"strings"
20: 
21: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/pipeline"
22: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/testdb"
23: 
24: 	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
25: )
26: 
27: func main() {
28: 	dsn := flag.String("dsn", testdb.DSN(), "CockroachDB connection string")
29: 	schema := flag.String("schema", "db/001_schema.sql", "path to the frozen DDL")
30: 	scenario := flag.String("scenario", "", "scenario ID (required for deterministic replay)")
31: 	reset := flag.Bool("reset", false, "drop and recreate the target database first")
32: 	out := flag.String("out", "", "transcript output path (default: stdout)")
33: 	flag.Parse()
34: 
35: 	if *dsn == "" {
36: 		fail("no DSN: pass --dsn or set FABLE_DSN")
37: 	}
38: 	if *scenario == "" {
39: 		fail("no scenario ID: pass --scenario (required for deterministic replay)")
40: 	}
41: 
42: 	fixtureDir := filepath.Join("internal", "derive", "testdata")
43: 	if _, err := os.Stat(fixtureDir); os.IsNotExist(err) {
44: 		fail("fixture directory not found: " + fixtureDir)
45: 	}
46: 
47: 	ctx := context.Background()
48: 
49: 	if *reset {
50: 		if err := testdb.Reset(ctx, *dsn, *schema); err != nil {
51: 			fail(fmt.Sprintf("reset: %v", err))
52: 		}
53: 	}
54: 
55: 	db, err := testdb.Open(*dsn)
56: 	if err != nil {
57: 		fail(fmt.Sprintf("open: %v", err))
58: 	}
59: 	defer func() { _ = db.Close() }()
60: 	if err := db.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
61: 		fail(fmt.Sprintf("ping: %v", err))
62: 	}
63: 
64: 	results, err := pipeline.Run(ctx, db, *scenario, fixtureDir)
65: 	if err != nil {
66: 		fail(fmt.Sprintf("pipeline: %v", err))
67: 	}
68: 
69: 	// Propose intents for promoted beliefs.
70: 	for _, r := range results {
71: 		if r.Contradiction || r.BeliefID == "" || !r.Promoted {
72: 			continue
73: 		}
74: 		if err := pipeline.ProposeIfNew(ctx, db, *scenario, r.BeliefID, "upgrade"); err != nil {
75: 			fail(fmt.Sprintf("propose intent: %v", err))
76: 		}
77: 	}
78: 
79: 	// Audit.
80: 	auditCount, err := pipeline.AuditIntent(ctx, db, *scenario)
81: 	if err != nil {
82: 		fail(fmt.Sprintf("audit: %v", err))
83: 	}
84: 
85: 	// Transcript.
86: 	var b strings.Builder
87: 	b.WriteString("SOLVENT TRANSCRIPT\n")
88: 	b.WriteString("==================\n\n")
89: 	b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("scenario: %s\n", *scenario))
90: 	b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("results:  %d\n\n", len(results)))
91: 
92: 	for i, r := range results {
93: 		b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("--- result %d ---\n", i+1))
94: 		b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  source:       %s\n", r.Normalized.SourceType))
95: 		b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  source_url:   %s\n", r.Normalized.SourceURL))
96: 
97: 		if r.Contradiction {
98: 			b.WriteString("  contradiction: true\n")
99: 			b.WriteString("  ledger_mutation: none\n")
100: 			b.WriteString("\n")
101: 			continue
102: 		}
103: 
104: 		for _, d := range r.Beliefs {
105: 			b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  claim:        %s\n", d.Claim))
106: 			b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  classification: %s\n", d.Classification))
107: 		}
108: 
109: 		if r.BeliefID != "" {
110: 			b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  belief_id:    %s\n", r.BeliefID))
111: 			b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  promoted:     %v\n", r.Promoted))
112: 			b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  debt_items:   %d\n", len(r.DebtItems)))
113: 			b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("  intent:       %s\n", intentState(r)))
114: 		}
115: 		b.WriteString("\n")
116: 	}
117: 
118: 	b.WriteString("--- audit ---\n")
119: 	b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("audit_live_on_nonpromoted: %d\n", auditCount))
120: 	b.WriteString("\n")
121: 
122: 	if auditCount != 0 {
123: 		b.WriteString("VERDICT: FAIL (audit ≠ 0)\n")
124: 	} else {
125: 		b.WriteString("VERDICT: PASS\n")
126: 	}
127: 
128: 	transcript := b.String()
129: 
130: 	if *out != "" {
131: 		if dir := filepath.Dir(*out); dir != "." {
132: 			if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
133: 				fail(fmt.Sprintf("mkdir: %v", err))
134: 			}
135: 		}
136: 		if err := os.WriteFile(*out, []byte(transcript), 0o644); err != nil {
137: 			fail(fmt.Sprintf("write: %v", err))
138: 		}
139: 		fmt.Printf("Transcript written to %s\n", *out)
140: 	} else {
141: 		fmt.Print(transcript)
142: 	}
143: }
144: 
145: func intentState(r pipeline.Result) string {
146: 	if r.IntentState != "" {
147: 		return r.IntentState
148: 	}
149: 	if r.Promoted {
150: 		return "live"
151: 	}
152: 	return ""
153: }
154: 
155: func fail(msg string) {
156: 	fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, msg)
157: 	os.Exit(1)
158: }

1: # WAVE4_IMPLEMENTATION.md
2: 
3: **Status:** Complete. All tests pass on CockroachDB v26.2.0.
4: **Scope:** Wave 4 — Minimum integration layer connecting evidence fixtures through Waves 1→2→3.
5: **Frozen waves:** Wave 1 (normalize), Wave 2 (derive), Wave 3 (belief, intent, kernel). No modifications.
6: **Schema:** `db/001_schema.sql` unchanged.
7: 
8: ---
9: 
10: ## 1. Files Created
11: 
12: | File | Lines | Purpose |
13: |---|---|---|
14: | `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go` | 243 | normalize→derive→belief.Process→ProposeIfNew chain with evidence aggregation |
15: | `internal/pipeline/pipeline_test.go` | 435 | 5 end-to-end integration tests |
16: | `cmd/solvent/main.go` | 136 | CLI orchestrator with deterministic transcript |
17: 
18: **Total:** 3 files, ~814 lines. No modifications to existing files.
19: 
20: ---
21: 
22: ## 2. Implementation Summary
23: 
24: ### Pipeline Architecture
25: 
26: ```
27: Phase 1: Derive (no DB writes)
28:   normalize.Normalize(raw, sourceType) → NormalizedEvidence
29:   derive.Derive(norm) → []DerivedBelief
30: 
31: Phase 2: Aggregate
32:   Merge all non-contradiction evidence into the primary vulnerability claim
33:   (first CVE-bearing claim). Contradictions collected separately.
34: 
35: Phase 3: Ledger
36:   belief.Process(ctx, db, scenarioID, mergedBelief)
37:     → EnsureBelief (find-or-create)
38:     → AddEvidence (deduplicated by content_sha256)
39:     → RetireDebt (mapping rules)
40:     → Promote (may block)
41: 
42: Phase 3.5: Intent
43:   ProposeIfNew(ctx, db, scenarioID, beliefID, "upgrade")
44:     → SELECT count(*) WHERE belief_id=X AND action=Y AND state='live'
45:     → intent.Propose (only if count = 0)
46: 
47: Phase 4: Contradictions
48:   Result{Contradiction: true, BeliefID: ""}
49:   slog.Warn with source_url
50:   NO ledger mutation
51: ```
52: 
53: ### Evidence Aggregation
54: 
55: The derive layer produces different claims for different evidence types:
56: - `kev_entry` → "etcd vX is vulnerable to CVE-XXXX"
57: - `release` → "fix available for etcd vX"
58: - `maintainer_comment` → "fix for etcd-io/etcd confirmed by maintainer"
59: - etc.
60: 
61: The pipeline's `aggregateDeriveResults` merges all non-contradiction evidence into the first CVE-bearing claim (the vulnerability belief). This ensures a single promoted belief with all supporting evidence attached, matching the plan's expected output.
62: 
63: ### Intent Idempotency
64: 
65: `ProposeIfNew` checks for an existing live intent before proposing:
66: 
67: ```sql
68: SELECT count(*) FROM action_intent
69: WHERE scenario_id=$1 AND belief_id=$2 AND action=$3 AND state='live'
70: ```
71: 
72: If count > 0, skips the INSERT. Replaying the same evidence does not create duplicate intents.
73: 
74: ---

78: | Test | Scenario ID | Purpose | Result |
79: |---|---|---|---|
80: | `TestEndToEnd_ProcessRealFixtures` | `55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000001` | Full pipeline: promote, audit=0, intent=live, idempotent replay | PASS |
81: | `TestEndToEnd_PartialDebtBlocksPromotion` | `55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000002` | Partial debt → entered, intent blocked | PASS |
82: | `TestEndToEnd_ContradictionNoLedgerMutation` | `55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000003` | Contradiction → 0 beliefs, 0 evidence | PASS |
83: | `TestEndToEnd_DeterministicResults` | `55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000004` / `...0005` | Deterministic fields identical across scenarios | PASS |
84: | `TestEndToEnd_ProposeIfNew_Idempotent` | `55555555-0000-0000-0000-000000000006` | 3x propose → 1 intent (idempotent) | PASS |
85: 
86: **5/5 tests pass.**
87: 
88: ---
89:

91:

115: ## 5. Deterministic Replay Result
116: 
117: Replaying the full pipeline against the same scenario produces identical deterministic state:
118: 
119: | Field | Run 1 | Run 2 | Match |
120: |---|---|---|---|
121: | Belief count | 1 | 1 | ✓ |
122: | Belief status | promoted | promoted | ✓ |
123: | Debt items | 0 | 0 | ✓ |
124: | Evidence count | 5 | 5 | ✓ |
125: | Audit count | 0 | 0 | ✓ |
126: | Intent count | 1 | 1 | ✓ |
127: 
128: ---
129: 
130: ## 6. Known Concurrency Limitation
131: 
132: **Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution; concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.**
133: 
134: The `ProposeIfNew` function uses a SELECT-then-INSERT pattern:
135: 
136: ```sql
137: -- Check
138: SELECT count(*) FROM action_intent
139: WHERE scenario_id=$1 AND belief_id=$2 AND action=$3 AND state='live'
140: 
141: -- Insert (only if count = 0)
142: INSERT INTO action_intent (scenario_id, belief_id, action) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)
143: ```
144: 
145: This is NOT a database-enforced concurrent uniqueness guarantee. Under concurrent execution, two goroutines could both see count=0 and both insert, creating duplicate live intents. This is acceptable for the MVP's sequential demo path. Concurrent duplicate-intent prevention would require a unique index or serializable transaction, which is deferred.
146: 
147: ---
148: 
149: ## 7. Known Limitations
150: 
151: 1. **Evidence aggregation is domain-specific.** The `aggregateDeriveResults` function merges all evidence into the first CVE-bearing claim. This is a pipeline-level convention for the etcd demo, not a kernel invariant. A multi-domain deployment would need a different aggregation strategy.
152: 
153: 2. **No live evidence feed.** The pipeline reads local JSON fixtures, not a live GitHub/CISA API. A real-data snapshot will be added after the pipeline works.
154: 
155: 3. **No graph visualization.** The dependency graph (Evidence → Belief → Action) is not rendered. Deferred.
156: 
157: 4. **No bchat integration.** Deferred.
158: 
159: 5. **Schema embedding column deferred.** `embedding VECTOR(1536) NULL` not yet in `db/001_schema.sql`. B-14 test not updated.
160: 
161: ---

1: // Package normalize transforms raw upstream events into the canonical
2: // NormalizedEvidence model. Every upstream source maps to this same structure.
3: //
4: // The normalization is deterministic: identical input always produces identical
5: // output and identical content_sha256. This is the deduplication guarantee.
6: package normalize

8: import "time"

10: // NormalizedEvidence is the domain-neutral transformation of a raw event.

12: type NormalizedEvidence struct {
13: 	ID              string    // content-addressed (SHA-256 of normalized form)
14: 	SourceURL       string    // canonical URL to the original observation
15: 	SourceType      string    // github_issue, github_pr, maintainer_comment, release, kev_entry
16: 	ContentSHA256   string    // hash of normalized content (for deduplication)
17: 	ObservedAt      time.Time // when observed upstream
18: 	IngestedAt      time.Time // when ingested into Solvent

21: 	Assertion       string    // what this claims (e.g., "contains fix for CVE-2024-12345")
22: 	Severity        string    // critical, high, medium, low, info
23: 	DomainPayload   []byte    // JSON blob with source-specific fields
24: }

1: // Package derive is the belief derivation engine.

4: // DerivedBelief values — pure semantic outputs describing what beliefs

18: 
19: // DerivedBelief describes a belief derived from evidence.
20: // Pure semantic output — no kernel operations, no debt, no IDs.
21: // Wave 3 decides what to do with these results.
22: type DerivedBelief struct {
23: 	// Claim is a structured factual statement about the world.
24: 	Claim string
25: 
26: 	// Classification identifies the belief's provenance.
27: 	// Derived: new belief from fresh evidence.
28: 	// Accommodated: evidence merged into or strengthening an existing belief.
29: 	Classification string
30: 
31: 	// SupportingEvidence is the evidence that supports this belief.
32: 	SupportingEvidence []normalize.NormalizedEvidence
33: 
34: 	// Contradicts is evidence that contradicts a prior belief.
35: 	// Non-empty only for contradiction results.
36: 	Contradicts []normalize.NormalizedEvidence
37: }

  "oldString": "// aggregateDeriveResults collects all fixture derive outputs and merges supporting\n// evidence from derived beliefs that reference the same vulnerability into a single\n// DerivedBelief. The first CVE-bearing claim becomes the primary claim; all other\n// non-contradiction evidence is merged into it as supporting evidence.\n// Contradictions are collected separately. Returns the merged beliefs and contradictions.\nfunc aggregateDeriveResults(allFixtureResults [][]derive.DerivedBelief) ([]derive.DerivedBelief, []derive.DerivedBelief) {\n\tcvePattern := regexp.MustCompile(`CVE-\\d{4}-\\d{4,}`)\n\n\t// Collect all claims and their evidence.\n\ttype claimEntry struct {\n\t\tclassification string\n\t\tevidence       []normalize.NormalizedEvidence\n\t}\n\tclaims := make(map[string]*claimEntry)\n\tvar contradictions []derive.DerivedBelief\n\n\t// Find the primary vulnerability claim (first CVE-bearing claim).\n\tvar primaryClaim string\n\n\tfor _, fixtureResults := range allFixtureResults {\n\t\tfor _, d := range fixtureResults {\n\t\t\tif len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tcontradictions = append(contradictions, d)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif d.Claim == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// If this claim has a CVE and we haven't set a primary, set it.\n\t\t\tif cvePattern.MatchString(d.Claim) && primaryClaim == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tprimaryClaim = d.Claim\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// If we have a primary claim, merge ALL other evidence into it.\n\t\t\t// This consolidates release, maintainer, and other supporting evidence\n\t\t\t// under the original vulnerability claim.\n\t\t\tif primaryClaim != \"\" && d.Claim != primaryClaim {\n\t\t\t\tif entry, ok := claims[primaryClaim]; ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tentry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tclaims[primaryClaim] = &claimEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tclassification: d.Classification,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tevidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Primary claim or no primary set yet: store normally.\n\t\t\tif entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tentry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tclaims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\tclassification: d.Classification,\n\t\t\t\t\tevidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar merged []derive.DerivedBelief\n\tfor claim, entry := range claims {\n\t\tmerged = append(merged, derive.DerivedBelief{\n\t\t\tClaim:              claim,\n\t\t\tClassification:     entry.classification,\n\t\t\tSupportingEvidence: entry.evidence,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn merged, contradictions\n}",
  "newString": "// mergedClaim pairs a DerivedBelief with the normalized provenance of its first\n// supporting evidence item. Used to populate Result.Normalized in Run().\ntype mergedClaim struct {\n\tbelief derive.DerivedBelief\n\tnorm   normalize.NormalizedEvidence\n}\n\n// aggregateDeriveResults collects all fixture derive outputs and merges supporting\n// evidence from derived beliefs that reference the same vulnerability into a single\n// DerivedBelief. Each distinct CVE-bearing claim remains independent — different\n// CVEs produce separate beliefs. Non-CVE evidence is merged into the first CVE-bearing\n// claim that matches its CVE identifier. Contradictions are collected separately.\n// Returns the merged beliefs (with provenance), sorted by claim for determinism,\n// and the contradictions.\nfunc aggregateDeriveResults(allFixtureResults [][]derive.DerivedBelief) ([]mergedClaim, []derive.DerivedBelief) {\n\tcvePattern := regexp.MustCompile(`CVE-\\d{4}-\\d{4,}`)\n\n\ttype claimEntry struct {\n\t\tclassification string\n\t\tevidence       []normalize.NormalizedEvidence\n\t\tnorm           normalize.NormalizedEvidence // provenance of first supporting evidence\n\t}\n\tclaims := make(map[string]*claimEntry)\n\tvar contradictions []derive.DerivedBelief\n\n\t// Track the first CVE seen — it becomes the primary for non-CVE evidence.\n\tvar primaryCVE string\n\n\tfor _, fixtureResults := range allFixtureResults {\n\t\tfor _, d := range fixtureResults {\n\t\t\tif len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tcontradictions = append(contradictions, d)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif d.Claim == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Extract CVE identifier from the claim, if any.\n\t\t\tcve := \"\"\n\t\t\tif m := cvePattern.FindString(d.Claim); m != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tcve = m\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// CVE-bearing claims are their own primaries.\n\t\t\tif cve != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif primaryCVE == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tprimaryCVE = cve\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// Same CVE as primary: merge evidence into the primary entry.\n\t\t\t\tif cve == primaryCVE {\n\t\t\t\t\tprimaryClaim := d.Claim\n\t\t\t\t\t// Find the existing primary entry — it may have different claim text.\n\t\t\t\t\tvar primaryEntry *claimEntry\n\t\t\t\t\tfor text, entry := range claims {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tif entryCVE := cvePattern.FindString(text); entryCVE == primaryCVE {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tprimaryEntry = entry\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t_ = text\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif primaryEntry != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tprimaryEntry.evidence = append(primaryEntry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)\n\t\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tclaims[primaryClaim] = &claimEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tclassification: d.Classification,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tevidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tnorm:           firstNorm(d),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t// Different CVE: independent claim.\n\t\t\t\tif entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tentry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tclaims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tclassification: d.Classification,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tevidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tnorm:           firstNorm(d),\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Non-CVE evidence: merge into the primary CVE claim if one exists.\n\t\t\tif primaryCVE != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tfor text, entry := range claims {\n\t\t\t\t\tif entryCVE := cvePattern.FindString(text); entryCVE == primaryCVE {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tentry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)\n\t\t\t\t\t\t_ = text\n\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// No CVE seen yet: store normally.\n\t\t\tif entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tentry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tclaims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{\n\t\t\t\t\tclassification: d.Classification,\n\t\t\t\t\tevidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,\n\t\t\t\t\tnorm:           firstNorm(d),\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Build sorted results for determinism (P5).\n\tvar sortedClaims []string\n\tfor claim := range claims {\n\t\tsortedClaims = append(sortedClaims, claim)\n\t}\n\tsort.Strings(sortedClaims)\n\n\tvar merged []mergedClaim\n\tfor _, claim := range sortedClaims {\n\t\tentry := claims[claim]\n\t\tmerged = append(merged, mergedClaim{\n\t\t\tbelief: derive.DerivedBelief{\n\t\t\t\tClaim:              claim,\n\t\t\t\tClassification:     entry.classification,\n\t\t\t\tSupportingEvidence: entry.evidence,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tnorm: entry.norm,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn merged, contradictions\n}\n\n// firstNorm returns the NormalizedEvidence from a DerivedBelief's first\n// supporting evidence item, or a zero value if none exists.\nfunc firstNorm(d derive.DerivedBelief) normalize.NormalizedEvidence {\n\tif len(d.SupportingEvidence) > 0 {\n\t\treturn d.SupportingEvidence[0]\n\t}\n\treturn normalize.NormalizedEvidence{}\n}"
}

\"55555555-0000-0000-0000-00000000000A\"\n\tscB := \"55555555-0000-0000-0000-00000000000B\"\n\n\tresultsA, err := pipeline.Run(ctx, shared, scA, tmpDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Run A: %v\", <content>
1: # WAVE4_REWORK.md
2: 
3: **Status:** Open — rework items documented but not applied to code.
4: **Scope:** `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go`, `internal/pipeline/pipeline_test.go`, `cmd/solvent/main.go`, documentation.
5: **Authority:** `WAVE4_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW.md` (F1–F4, P4, P5), TL rework reviews (`wave4_rework.md`, `wave4_rework2.md`).
6: **Method:** Code audit against adversarial review findings; verified against live CockroachDB v26.2.0.
7: 
8: ---
9: 
10: ## Verdict: REWORK REQUIRED
11: 
12: Six rework items were identified by the adversarial review and documented. The code fixes were **never implemented**. The current `pipeline.go`, `pipeline_test.go`, and `main.go` still contain the unfixed versions. All findings are contained to Wave 4 files — no schema changes, no frozen wave modifications.
13: 
14: ---
15: 
16: ## Finding Status
17: 
18: ### F1 [P1] — Contradiction path is silent in `Run()` — no `slog.Warn`
19: 
20: **Spec violated:** D-6 — "Contradiction fixture produces `slog.Warn` … transcript shows warning with `source_url`."
21: **Location:** `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go:289-295` (Phase 4 of `Run`).
22: **Current code:**
23: ```go
24: // Phase 4: record contradictions (no ledger mutation).
25: for _, c := range contradictions {
26:     allResults = append(allResults, Result{
27:         Contradiction: true,
28:         Beliefs:       []derive.DerivedBelief{c},
29:     })
30: }
31: ```
32: **Problem:** Zero log lines emitted. The `ProcessEvidence` path (line 85-96) does warn, but `Run()` — the path `cmd/solvent` actually uses — does not. The demo-facing contradiction receipt is silent.
33: **Fix:** In `Run` Phase 4, emit `slog.Warn` with `source_url` and `source_type` before appending the result. Consider refactoring so both `ProcessEvidence` and `Run` reuse the same warn helper.
34: 
35: ---
36: 
37: ### F2 [P1] — `Result.Normalized` empty in `Run()`, transcript source fields always blank
38: 
39: **Spec violated:** Plan §13 — source type and `source_url` are deterministic transcript fields.
40: **Location:** `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go:270-275` (result construction in `Run`).
41: **Current code:**
42: ```go
43: allResults = append(allResults, Result{
44:     BeliefID:  beliefID,
45:     Promoted:  status == "promoted",
46:     DebtItems: make([]string, debtLen),
47:     Beliefs:   []derive.DerivedBelief{d},
48: })
49: ```
50: **Problem:** `Normalized` is left zero-valued. `cmd/solvent/main.go:94-95` renders `r.Normalized.SourceType` and `r.Normalized.SourceURL` — both always empty in the demo path.
51: **Fix:** Set `Normalized` from the `norm` produced in Phase 1. The `norm` variable is scoped inside the Phase 1 loop; either carry it through the aggregation or store it on the merged `DerivedBelief`. ~3 lines.
52: 
53: ---
54: 
55: ### F4 [P1] — Evidence aggregation merges semantically independent CVE claims
56: 
57: **Spec violated:** N1 — "verify that this cannot accidentally merge semantically independent claims."
58: **Location:** `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go:147-165` (`aggregateDeriveResults`).
59: **Current code:**
60: ```go
61: if primaryClaim != "" && d.Claim != primaryClaim {
62:     // merge into primary — no CVE check
63: }
64: ```
65: **Problem:** A second CVE-bearing claim (e.g., "redis is vulnerable to CVE-2024-00001") is silently merged into the first CVE claim (e.g., "etcd is vulnerable to CVE-2024-24790"), its evidence attached to a belief about a different product and vulnerability. The current single-CVE fixture set masks this.
66: **Fix:** CVE-bearing claims are their own primaries; never merge a different-CVE claim into an existing primary:
67: ```go
68: if cvePattern.MatchString(d.Claim) {
69:     // CVE-bearing claims are their own primaries
70:     if entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {
71:         entry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
72:     } else {
73:         claims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{
74:             classification: d.Classification,
75:             evidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,
76:         }
77:     }
78:     continue
79: }
80: if primaryClaim != "" {
81:     // merge non-CVE evidence into primary
82: }
83: ```
84: 
85: ---
86: 
87: ### F3 [P2] — Documentation inconsistency on embedding column
88: 
89: **Problem:** Some doc references mention `belief.embedding` as if it exists. The schema (`db/001_schema.sql`) has no embedding column by design (I-6).
90: **Location:** `WAVE4_IMPLEMENTATION.md:159` — says `embedding VECTOR(1536) NULL` not yet in schema. This is accurate but inconsistent with other references.
91: **Fix:** Ensure all documentation says: "The schema has no embedding column by design (I-6). The ledger operates correctly with zero vectors." No code change.
92: 
93: ---
94: 
95: ### P4 [P2] — Test count discrepancy: 80 pass, not 81
96: 
97: **Receipt claims:** kernel=28, pipeline=7, total=81.
98: **Actual counts:**
99: | Package | Tests |
100: |---|---|
101: | `internal/kernel` | 27 |
102: | `internal/belief` | 18 |
103: | `internal/derive` | 16 |
104: | `internal/normalize` | 11 |
105: | `internal/intent` | 3 |
106: | `internal/pipeline` | 5 |
107: | **Total** | **80** |
108: 
109: **Fix:** Update all receipt tables in `WAVE4_IMPLEMENTATION.md` and any other docs to reflect actual counts.
110: 
111: ---
112: 
113: ### P5 [P3] — Result-order determinism depends on Go map iteration order
114: 
115: **Location:** `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go:179-186` (`aggregateDeriveResults`).
116: **Current code:**
117: ```go
118: for claim, entry := range claims {
119:     merged = append(merged, derive.DerivedBelief{...})
120: }
121: ```
122: **Problem:** Map iteration order in Go is unspecified. Currently one merged claim so order holds, but `TestEndToEnd_DeterministicResults` would flake with two claims.
123: **Fix:** Sort `merged` by claim before return (~2 lines).
124: 
125: ---
126: 
127: ## Additional Finding (not in adversarial review)
128: 
129: ### main.go redundant intent proposal
130: 
131: **Location:** `cmd/solvent/main.go:70-77`.
132: **Current code:**
133: ```go
134: // Propose intents for promoted beliefs.
135: for _, r := range results {
136:     if r.Contradiction || r.BeliefID == "" || !r.Promoted {
137:         continue
138:     }
139:     if err := pipeline.ProposeIfNew(ctx, db, *scenario, r.BeliefID, "upgrade"); err != nil {
140:         fail(fmt.Sprintf("propose intent: %v", err))
141:     }
142: }
143: ```
144: **Problem:** `Run()` Phase 3.5 already proposes intents and sets `r.IntentState = "live"`. This loop re-proposes — redundant. `ProposeIfNew` is idempotent (SELECT-then-INSERT), so it's harmless but wasteful.
145: **Recommendation:** Remove the redundant loop from `main.go`. The transcript already reads `r.IntentState` from `Run()`.

```
<path>/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/internal/pipeline/pipeline.go</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: // Package pipeline is the minimum integration layer for Wave 4.
2: //
3: // It chains normalize → derive → belief.Process → ProposeIfNew → intent.Audit
4: // into a single pass over evidence fixtures. The kernel owns correctness;
5: // this package is a thin orchestrator.
6: //
7: // Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution;
8: // concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.
9: package pipeline
10: 
11: import (
12: 	"context"
13: 	"database/sql"
14: 	"errors"
15: 	"fmt"
16: 	"log/slog"
17: 	"os"
18: 	"path/filepath"
19: 	"regexp"
20: 	"sort"
21: 
22: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/belief"
23: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/derive"
24: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/intent"
25: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/kernel"
26: 	"github.com/PithomLabs/solvent/internal/normalize"
27: )
28: 
29: // Result captures the outcome of processing one evidence item through the pipeline.
30: type Result struct {
31: 	Normalized    normalize.NormalizedEvidence
32: 	Beliefs       []derive.DerivedBelief
33: 	BeliefID      string // empty if contradiction or no belief emitted
34: 	Promoted      bool
35: 	DebtItems     []string // remaining debt after processing
36: 	IntentState   string   // "live", "cancelled", or "" (no intent proposed)
37: 	Contradiction bool     // true if this result is a contradiction (no ledger mutation)
38: }
39: 
40: // sourceTypeMap maps fixture filenames to their source types.
41: // The filename-to-source-type mapping is deterministic and compile-time.
42: var sourceTypeMap = map[string]string{
43: 	"kev_entry.json":                normalize.SourceKEVEntry,
44: 	"kev_entry_v2.json":             normalize.SourceKEVEntry,
45: 	"release_with_fix.json":         normalize.SourceRelease,
46: 	"maintainer_fixes.json":         normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
47: 	"maintainer_no_regression.json": normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
48: 	"maintainer_reviewed.json":      normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
49: 	"release_confirmed.json":        normalize.SourceRelease,
50: 	"contradicting_evidence.json":   normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
51: 	"non_maintainer_comment.json":   normalize.SourceMaintainerComment,
52: 	"unmerged_pr.json":              normalize.SourceGitHubPR,
53: }
54: 
55: // ProcessEvidence chains normalize → derive → belief.Process for one raw evidence item.
56: //
57: // Normal path:
58: //
59: //	normalize → derive → belief.Process (EnsureBelief, AddEvidence, RetireDebt, Promote)
60: //	→ Result with BeliefID set, Contradiction=false
61: //
62: // Contradiction path (DerivedBelief.Contradicts non-empty):
63: //
64: //	→ Result with BeliefID="", Contradiction=true
65: //	→ slog.Warn emitted with source_url
66: //	→ NO ledger mutation (belief.Process returns nil without DB writes)
67: func ProcessEvidence(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string, raw []byte, sourceType string) ([]Result, error) {
68: 	norm, err := normalize.Normalize(raw, sourceType)
69: 	if err != nil {
70: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: normalize: %w", err)
71: 	}
72: 
73: 	derived := derive.Derive(norm)
74: 	if len(derived) == 0 {
75: 		return nil, nil
76: 	}
77: 
78: 	var results []Result
79: 	for _, d := range derived {
80: 		r := Result{
81: 			Normalized: norm,
82: 			Beliefs:    []derive.DerivedBelief{d},
83: 		}
84: 
85: 		// Contradiction path: no ledger mutation.
86: 		if len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {
87: 			r.Contradiction = true
88: 			for _, c := range d.Contradicts {
89: 				slog.Warn("pipeline: contradiction received, no ledger mutation",
90: 					"source_url", c.SourceURL,
91: 					"source_type", c.SourceType,
92: 					"claim", d.Claim,
93: 				)
94: 			}
95: 			results = append(results, r)
96: 			continue
97: 		}
98: 
99: 		// Normal path: belief.Process → kernel operations.
100: 		if err := belief.Process(ctx, db, scenarioID, d); err != nil {
101: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: belief.Process: %w", err)
102: 		}
103: 
104: 		// Query the belief ID and state.
105: 		beliefID, status, debtLen, err := queryBelief(ctx, db, scenarioID, d.Claim)
106: 		if err != nil {
107: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: query belief: %w", err)
108: 		}
109: 
110: 		r.BeliefID = beliefID
111: 		r.Promoted = status == "promoted"
112: 		r.DebtItems = make([]string, debtLen)
113: 		results = append(results, r)
114: 	}
115: 
116: 	return results, nil
117: }
118: 
119: // mergedClaim pairs a DerivedBelief with the normalized provenance of its first
120: // supporting evidence item. Used to populate Result.Normalized in Run().
121: type mergedClaim struct {
122: 	belief derive.DerivedBelief
123: 	norm   normalize.NormalizedEvidence
124: }
125: 
126: // aggregateDeriveResults collects all fixture derive outputs and merges supporting
127: // evidence from derived beliefs that reference the same vulnerability into a single
128: // DerivedBelief. Each distinct CVE-bearing claim remains independent — different
129: // CVEs produce separate beliefs. Non-CVE evidence is merged into the first CVE-bearing
130: // claim that matches its CVE identifier. Contradictions are collected separately.
131: // Returns the merged beliefs (with provenance), sorted by claim for determinism,
132: // and the contradictions.
133: func aggregateDeriveResults(allFixtureResults [][]derive.DerivedBelief) ([]mergedClaim, []derive.DerivedBelief) {
134: 	cvePattern := regexp.MustCompile(`CVE-\d{4}-\d{4,}`)
135: 
136: 	type claimEntry struct {
137: 		classification string
138: 		evidence       []normalize.NormalizedEvidence
139: 		norm           normalize.NormalizedEvidence // provenance of first supporting evidence
140: 	}
141: 	claims := make(map[string]*claimEntry)
142: 	var contradictions []derive.DerivedBelief
143: 
144: 	// Track the first CVE seen — it becomes the primary for non-CVE evidence.
145: 	var primaryCVE string
146: 
147: 	for _, fixtureResults := range allFixtureResults {
148: 		for _, d := range fixtureResults {
149: 			if len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {
150: 				contradictions = append(contradictions, d)
151: 				continue
152: 			}
153: 			if d.Claim == "" {
154: 				continue
155: 			}
156: 
157: 			// Extract CVE identifier from the claim, if any.
158: 			cve := ""
159: 			if m := cvePattern.FindString(d.Claim); m != "" {
160: 				cve = m
161: 			}
162: 
163: 			// CVE-bearing claims are their own primaries.
164: 			if cve != "" {
165: 				if primaryCVE == "" {
166: 					primaryCVE = cve
167: 				}
168: 				// Same CVE as primary: merge evidence into the primary entry.
169: 				if cve == primaryCVE {
170: 					primaryClaim := d.Claim
171: 					// Find the existing primary entry — it may have different claim text.
172: 					var primaryEntry *claimEntry
173: 					for text, entry := range claims {
174: 						if entryCVE := cvePattern.FindString(text); entryCVE == primaryCVE {
175: 							primaryEntry = entry
176: 							_ = text
177: 							break
178: 						}
179: 					}
180: 					if primaryEntry != nil {
181: 						primaryEntry.evidence = append(primaryEntry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
182: 					} else {
183: 						claims[primaryClaim] = &claimEntry{
184: 							classification: d.Classification,
185: 							evidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,
186: 							norm:           firstNorm(d),
187: 						}
188: 					}
189: 					continue
190: 				}
191: 				// Different CVE: independent claim.
192: 				if entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {
193: 					entry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
194: 				} else {
195: 					claims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{
196: 						classification: d.Classification,
197: 						evidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,
198: 						norm:           firstNorm(d),
199: 					}
200: 				}
201: 				continue
202: 			}
203: 
204: 			// Non-CVE evidence: merge into the primary CVE claim if one exists.
205: 			if primaryCVE != "" {
206: 				for text, entry := range claims {
207: 					if entryCVE := cvePattern.FindString(text); entryCVE == primaryCVE {
208: 						entry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
209: 						_ = text
210: 						break
211: 					}
212: 				}
213: 				continue
214: 			}
215: 
216: 			// No CVE seen yet: store normally.
217: 			if entry, ok := claims[d.Claim]; ok {
218: 				entry.evidence = append(entry.evidence, d.SupportingEvidence...)
219: 			} else {
220: 				claims[d.Claim] = &claimEntry{
221: 					classification: d.Classification,
222: 					evidence:       d.SupportingEvidence,
223: 					norm:           firstNorm(d),
224: 				}
225: 			}
226: 		}
227: 	}
228: 
229: 	// Build sorted results for determinism (P5).
230: 	var sortedClaims []string
231: 	for claim := range claims {
232: 		sortedClaims = append(sortedClaims, claim)
233: 	}
234: 	sort.Strings(sortedClaims)
235: 
236: 	var merged []mergedClaim
237: 	for _, claim := range sortedClaims {
238: 		entry := claims[claim]
239: 		merged = append(merged, mergedClaim{
240: 			belief: derive.DerivedBelief{
241: 				Claim:              claim,
242: 				Classification:     entry.classification,
243: 				SupportingEvidence: entry.evidence,
244: 			},
245: 			norm: entry.norm,
246: 		})
247: 	}
248: 
249: 	return merged, contradictions
250: }
251: 
252: // firstNorm returns the NormalizedEvidence from a DerivedBelief's first
253: // supporting evidence item, or a zero value if none exists.
254: func firstNorm(d derive.DerivedBelief) normalize.NormalizedEvidence {
255: 	if len(d.SupportingEvidence) > 0 {
256: 		return d.SupportingEvidence[0]
257: 	}
258: 	return normalize.NormalizedEvidence{}
259: }
260: 
261: // ProposeIfNew records a live action intent on a promoted belief only if no live intent
262: // already exists for the same belief/action pair. Idempotent: replaying the same evidence
263: // does not create duplicate intents.
264: //
265: // Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution;
266: // concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.
267: func ProposeIfNew(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID, beliefID, action string) error {
268: 	var count int
269: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
270: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM action_intent
271: 		 WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID AND belief_id=$2::UUID AND action=$3::STRING AND state='live'`,
272: 		scenarioID, beliefID, action).Scan(&count)
273: 	if err != nil {
274: 		return fmt.Errorf("pipeline: check existing intent: %w", err)
275: 	}
276: 	if count > 0 {
277: 		return nil
278: 	}
279: 	return intent.Propose(ctx, db, scenarioID, beliefID, action)
280: }
281: 
282: // Run processes a directory of evidence fixtures in deterministic order.
283: // Returns results in processing order.
284: func Run(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string, fixtureDir string) ([]Result, error) {
285: 	entries, err := os.ReadDir(fixtureDir)
286: 	if err != nil {
287: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: read fixture dir: %w", err)
288: 	}
289: 
290: 	// Deterministic order: sort by filename.
291: 	var filenames []string
292: 	for _, e := range entries {
293: 		if e.IsDir() {
294: 			continue
295: 		}
296: 		if filepath.Ext(e.Name()) != ".json" {
297: 			continue
298: 		}
299: 		filenames = append(filenames, e.Name())
300: 	}
301: 	sort.Strings(filenames)
302: 
303: 	// Phase 1: derive all fixture evidence (no DB writes).
304: 	var allFixtureResults [][]derive.DerivedBelief
305: 	for _, name := range filenames {
306: 		raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(fixtureDir, name))
307: 		if err != nil {
308: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: read fixture %s: %w", name, err)
309: 		}
310: 
311: 		sourceType, ok := sourceTypeMap[name]
312: 		if !ok {
313: 			continue
314: 		}
315: 
316: 		norm, err := normalize.Normalize(raw, sourceType)
317: 		if err != nil {
318: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: normalize %s: %w", name, err)
319: 		}
320: 
321: 		allFixtureResults = append(allFixtureResults, derive.Derive(norm))
322: 	}
323: 
324: 	// Phase 2: aggregate evidence by claim.
325: 	merged, contradictions := aggregateDeriveResults(allFixtureResults)
326: 
327: 	// Phase 3: process aggregated beliefs through the ledger.
328: 	var allResults []Result
329: 
330: 	for _, mc := range merged {
331: 		d := mc.belief
332: 		if err := belief.Process(ctx, db, scenarioID, d); err != nil {
333: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: belief.Process: %w", err)
334: 		}
335: 
336: 		beliefID, status, debtLen, err := queryBelief(ctx, db, scenarioID, d.Claim)
337: 		if err != nil {
338: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: query belief: %w", err)
339: 		}
340: 
341: 		allResults = append(allResults, Result{
342: 			Normalized: mc.norm,
343: 			BeliefID:   beliefID,
344: 			Promoted:   status == "promoted",
345: 			DebtItems:  make([]string, debtLen),
346: 			Beliefs:    []derive.DerivedBelief{d},
347: 		})
348: 	}
349: 
350: 	// Phase 3.5: propose intents for promoted beliefs.
351: 	for i := range allResults {
352: 		r := &allResults[i]
353: 		if r.Promoted && r.BeliefID != "" {
354: 			if err := ProposeIfNew(ctx, db, scenarioID, r.BeliefID, "upgrade"); err != nil {
355: 				return nil, fmt.Errorf("pipeline: propose intent: %w", err)
356: 			}
357: 			r.IntentState = "live"
358: 		}
359: 	}
360: 
361: 	// Phase 4: record contradictions with warning (F1). No ledger mutation.
362: 	for _, c := range contradictions {
363: 		for _, ev := range c.Contradicts {
364: 			slog.Warn("pipeline: contradiction received, no ledger mutation",
365: 				"source_url", ev.SourceURL,
366: 				"source_type", ev.SourceType,
367: 				"claim", c.Claim,
368: 			)
369: 		}
370: 		contradictionNorm := firstNorm(c)
371: 		if contradictionNorm.SourceType == "" && len(c.Contradicts) > 0 {
372: 			contradictionNorm = c.Contradicts[0]
373: 		}
374: 		allResults = append(allResults, Result{
375: 			Normalized:    contradictionNorm,
376: 			Contradiction: true,
377: 			Beliefs:       []derive.DerivedBelief{c},
378: 		})
379: 	}
380: 
381: 	return allResults, nil
382: }
383: 
384: // queryBelief returns the belief ID, status, and remaining debt count for a claim.
385: func queryBelief(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID, claim string) (id, status string, debtLen int, err error) {
386: 	err = db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
387: 		`SELECT id, status, coalesce(array_length(debt,1),0)
388: 		 FROM belief WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID AND claim=$2`,
389: 		scenarioID, claim).Scan(&id, &status, &debtLen)
390: 	if err != nil {
391: 		return "", "", 0, err
392: 	}
393: 	return id, status, debtLen, nil
394: }
395: 
396: // AuditIntent returns the audit count for the scenario.
397: func AuditIntent(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
398: 	return intent.Audit(ctx, db, scenarioID)
399: }
400: 
401: // QueryBeliefCount returns the number of beliefs in the scenario.
402: func QueryBeliefCount(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
403: 	var count int
404: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
405: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM belief WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID`, scenarioID).Scan(&count)
406: 	return count, err
407: }
408: 
409: // QueryEvidenceCount returns the number of evidence rows in the scenario.
410: func QueryEvidenceCount(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
411: 	var count int
412: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
413: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM evidence WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID`, scenarioID).Scan(&count)
414: 	return count, err
415: }
416: 
417: // QueryIntentCount returns the number of live intents in the scenario.
418: func QueryIntentCount(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
419: 	var count int
420: 	err := db.QueryRowContext(ctx,
421: 		`SELECT count(*) FROM action_intent WHERE scenario_id=$1::UUID AND state='live'`, scenarioID).Scan(&count)
422: 	return count, err
423: }
424: 
425: // IsActionOnUnpromoted reports whether err is ErrActionOnUnpromoted.
426: func IsActionOnUnpromoted(err error) bool {
427: 	return errors.Is(err, kernel.ErrActionOnUnpromoted)
428: }

(End of file - total 428 lines)
</content>

1: # AGENTS.md
2: 
3: Think like a distributed systems engineer, not an LLM engineer.
4: 
5: ## Project
6: 
7: **Solvent** is a transactional belief ledger for autonomous agents.
8: 
9: The primary objective is **not** to build another AI agent or chatbot.
10: 
11: The objective is to build a **database-enforced memory system** where autonomous agents cannot act on beliefs that are no longer true.
12: 
13: The demo is only a presentation of this capability.
14: 
15: ---
16: 
17: # Core Thesis
18: 
19: Traditional agent memory stores facts.
20: 
21: Solvent stores **beliefs**.
22: 
23: Beliefs:
24: 
25: - carry evidence,
26: - evolve as new evidence arrives,
27: - may be promoted or retracted,
28: - govern whether an agent may act.
29: 
30: The database—not the LLM—determines whether an action is allowed.
31: 
32: ---
33: 
34: # Problem Statement
35: 
36: Modern AI agents often continue acting on stale information.
37: 
38: Prompt engineering cannot reliably prevent this.
39: 
40: Application code alone cannot reliably enforce correctness under concurrency.
41: 
42: Solvent applies distributed systems principles to agent memory.
43: 
44: Beliefs become transactional objects.
45: 
46: Actions become database-gated.
47: 
48: ---
49: 
50: # Primary Design Principle
51: 
52: > Memory is not remembering.
53: >
54: > Memory is refusing to act on what is no longer true.
55: 
56: This principle takes precedence over convenience or agent autonomy.
57: 
58: ---
59: 
60: # Architectural Principles
61: 
62: The architecture is frozen.
63: 
64: Do not redesign.
65: 
66: Current MVP consists of only:
67: 
68: - belief
69: - evidence
70: - action_intent
71: 
72: No additional tables unless explicitly approved.
73: 
74: The kernel remains domain-agnostic.
75: 
76: ---
77: 
78: # Evidence
79: 
80: The kernel never depends on a specific domain.
81: 
82: Cybersecurity, etcd, GitHub, package advisories, logistics, etc. are merely **EvidenceFeed** implementations.
83: 
84: The current MVP uses a real etcd issue history as demonstration data.
85: 
86: Changing the evidence source must not require kernel changes.
87: 
88: ---
89: 
90: # Agent Responsibilities
91: 
92: Agents do not decide truth.
93: 
94: Agents:
95: 
96: - observe evidence,
97: - propose beliefs,
98: - propose actions.
99: 
100: The Transactional Belief Ledger determines whether those actions remain valid.
101: 
102: ---
103: 
104: # Database Responsibilities
105: 
106: CockroachDB is not passive storage.
107: 
108: CockroachDB enforces:
109: 
110: - transactional consistency,
111: - promotion rules,
112: - action gating,
113: - atomic retraction,
114: - auditability.
115: 
116: Whenever possible, correctness belongs in schema and transactions rather than application code.
117: 
118: ---
119: 
120: # Engineering Philosophy
121: 
122: Prefer:
123: 
124: - explicit invariants
125: - receipts
126: - deterministic behavior
127: - transactional correctness
128: - minimal architecture
129: 
130: Avoid:
131: 
132: - hidden state
133: - prompt-only guarantees
134: - duplicated truth
135: - speculative abstractions
136: - unnecessary orchestration
137: 
138: ---
139: 
140: # Scope
141: 
142: This repository builds the **Transactional Belief Ledger**.
143: 
144: The demonstration application exists solely to make the ledger understandable.
145: 
146: If a design decision benefits the demo but weakens the ledger, reject it.
147: 
148: If a design decision strengthens the ledger but simplifies the demo, prefer the ledger.
149: 
150: The ledger is the product.
151: 
152: The demo is the proof.
153: 
154: ---
155: 
156: # Development Rules
157: 
158: - Preserve the frozen architecture.
159: - Do not silently redesign behavior.
160: - Unknowns become receipts.
161: - Implementation follows approved specifications.
162: - Database correctness takes precedence over application convenience.
163: 
164: When in doubt, ask:
165: 
166: > "Should this responsibility belong to the database instead?"

<content>
1: # README.md
2: 
3: Standing context for AI agents working in this repo. Read this before editing code.
4: This is orientation, not a task list — the milestone plan lives in `IMPLEMENTATION_CONTRACT.md`.
5: 
6: ## What this is (primary)
7: 
8: This repository is a **transactional belief ledger for autonomous agents**. It is not a
9: chatbot, a RAG store, or a security tool. Its job is to hold *claims* as first-class
10: database rows that carry their evidence, their typed provenance, their unpaid epistemic
11: obligations ("debt"), their dependents, and their promotion state — and to make a claim
12: **actionable only when it has earned it**, with that gate enforced by the database schema
13: rather than by any prompt. When new evidence falsifies a claim, every decision downstream
14: of it is un-promoted in one atomic transaction. Concurrent agents revising the same belief
15: is a write-skew problem; serializable isolation is why the substrate is CockroachDB.
16: 
17: The mental model, in five lines (all load-bearing):
18: - **Ideas enter free.** A belief is written cheaply, fully in debt, `status='entered'`.
19: - **Promotion costs debt.** A belief becomes actionable only when its debt is empty.
20: - **New evidence creates new debt.** Falsification is debt reappearing, not deletion.
21: - **Debt does not kill.** Retracted beliefs are preserved — this is the audit story.
22: - **Knowledge oscillates, it does not accumulate.** Memory here is *subtractive*.
23: 
24: One sentence for humans: *beliefs enter free; actions cost promotion; the gate is in the tables.*
25: 
26: ## Invariants — never violate these
27: 
28: - **I-1** No `promoted` belief has non-empty `debt`. (schema CHECK `promoted_is_debt_free`)
29: - **I-2** No `promoted` belief has `final_truth = true`. (same CHECK)
30: - **I-3** No `live` action_intent references a non-`promoted` belief. (composite FK + CHECK)
31: - **I-4** A belief with a live intent cannot become `retracted` until that intent is cancelled.
32:   (composite FK `ON UPDATE CASCADE` propagates status into the intent, then the CHECK fires)
33: - **I-5** `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted` returns 0 in every committed state.
34: - **I-6** Every invariant holds with all `embedding` columns NULL. Vectors are an optimization,
35:   never part of belief semantics.
36: - **I-7** Every kernel write goes through `crdb.ExecuteTx`. No raw `db.Exec`/`db.Query` writes.
37: - **I-8** `RetractCascade` is a single transaction; cancel-before-retract ordering is mandatory.
38: 
39: If a change would require weakening any of I-1..I-8, it is out of scope — stop and report.
40: 
41: ## Enforcement locus — the thing agents get wrong
42: 
43: **I-1 through I-4 are enforced by the database, not by Go.** They live in CHECK constraints,
44: a composite `UNIQUE (id, status)` target, and a `FOREIGN KEY (belief_id, belief_status) …
45: ON UPDATE CASCADE`. Do **not** re-implement these as application-level guards in Go. Duplicated
46: guards drift from the schema, contradict it under concurrency, and give a false sense of safety.
47: The kernel's job is to *route writes through transactions* and *translate the database's refusal
48: into a typed Go error* — not to pre-check what the schema already refuses. The whole thesis is
49: that the gate is structural; keep it structural.
50: 
51: ## Architecture at a glance
52: 
53: - **Schema (frozen):** four tables in `db/001_schema.sql` — `belief`, `belief_edge`,
54:   `evidence`, `action_intent`. Do not modify this file (see boundaries).
55:   - `claim_type` ∈ {`derived`,`accommodated`,`postulated`}; `status` ∈
56:     {`entered`,`promoted`,`retracted`} — there is no fourth status. `debt` is `TEXT[]`.
57:   - `evidence.provenance_class` ∈ {`external_feed`,`reproducible_artifact`,`live_scan`,
58:     `operator_asserted`}; `content_sha256` is required.
59: - **Kernel:** `internal/kernel` (package `kernel`). Public surface, behaviour only:
60:   `New`, `EnterBelief`, `AddEvidence`, `RetireDebt`, `Promote` (→ `ErrPromotionBlocked`),
61:   `IntentOnPromoted` (→ `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`), `RetractCascade`, `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted`,
62:   plus `FullDebt` and the `ClaimType` constants. This package must not import demo/domain code.
63: - **Substrate:** CockroachDB. `cockroach-go/v2` (`crdb.ExecuteTx`).
64: - **Agents (thin):** a *claim agent* (ingest evidence → enter/type claims → retire debt →
65:   merge duplicates by vector similarity before insert) and a *security agent* (read promoted
66:   beliefs → write intents). Keep them dumb; the memory layer is the intelligence.
67: 
68: ## Go + CockroachDB conventions
69: 
70: - **All writes via `crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, db, nil, func(tx) error { … })`.** It runs the
71:   40001 retry/savepoint protocol. A write outside it is a bug (I-7).
72: - **Classify errors by SQLSTATE, never by string.** Use `errors.As` to reach the pgx
73:   `SQLState()`; `23514` = CHECK violation (→ `ErrPromotionBlocked`), `23503` = FK violation
74:   (→ `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`), `40001` = serialization failure (let `ExecuteTx` retry).
75: - **Serializable is the default on CockroachDB, and that default is the point.** Do not reach
76:   for `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` as the primary safety mechanism — explicit locks are per-call-site
77:   vigilance that every future edit must remember; serializable requires remembering nothing.
78:   `READ COMMITTED` appears **only** in the isolation experiment, deliberately, to contrast.
79: - **Confidence is derived, never stored.** There is no confidence column and must never be one.
80:   If a UI needs a legibility signal, derive it (e.g. count of open debt); never persist a scalar.
81: - **The cascade is one transaction:** recursive walk of `belief_edge` from the root, cancel live
82:   intents on all descendants, then un-promote them — in that order, in a single `ExecuteTx`.
83: - **Vector column is nullable and used for merge-not-fork** (dedup on write), not retrieval.
84:   The ledger must be fully correct with it NULL (I-6).
85: 
86: ## Hard boundaries (do not cross without escalation)
87: 
88: - **The schema is frozen.** Do not edit `db/001_schema.sql`. A needed schema change is a
89:   blocking finding to report, not an edit to make.
90: - **No new tables, agents, or evidence feeds.** The system is deliberately three-tables-two-
91:   agents-one-feed-one-graph. Additions belong in a post-hackathon backlog.
92: - **Kernel stays domain-agnostic.** No feed name (KEV, GitHub, etcd, …) appears in
93:   `internal/kernel`. Domain lives in the ingestor and the demo only.
94: - **No fabricated evidence — ever.** No invented IDs, advisories, or URLs. `external_feed`
95:   rows must have a resolving `source_url` and a matching `content_sha256`; CI enforces this.
96:   In a system whose entire pitch is provenance, fabricated evidence is self-refuting.
97: - **No internal vocabulary on user-facing surfaces.** Words like `accommodated`/`postulated`/
98:   `derived` are schema values, not narration; they stay out of the demo and any UI copy.
99: 
100: ## The demo (secondary)
101: 
102: The demo is **Layer 4 and swappable** — it exists to make the kernel legible, and the kernel
103: must never depend on it. The current domain candidate is a live software-project belief stream
104: (GitHub-issue timelines; etcd is the leading choice for its dated, public "declared stable →
105: publicly retracted" oscillation). Whatever the domain, the demo must prove exactly three things,
106: all backed by real receipts: the **gate** (an action on an unpromoted belief is refused by the
107: FK, raw error shown), the **cascade** (new evidence falsifies a belief and downstream intents
108: cancel atomically), and the **three-cell isolation experiment** (naive schema @ READ COMMITTED →
109: silent corruption; @ SERIALIZABLE → 40001 refusal; hardened schema @ READ COMMITTED → FK refusal).
110: Swapping the domain changes the ingestor and the narrative; it changes nothing above Layer 4.
111: 
112: ## Before you commit
113: 
114: - `go build ./...` and `go vet ./...` clean; no write path outside `crdb.ExecuteTx`.
115: - Kernel invariant tests green — and green a second time with all embeddings NULL (I-6).
116: - The pure-SQL proof still passes: `proof/02_lifecycle_and_invariants.sql` and the isolation
117:   harness in `proof/`. These are the source of truth the Go must reproduce, not diverge from.
118: 
119: ## Escalation
120: 
121: If the schema cannot support a required behaviour, if an invariant would have to be relaxed,
122: or if the composite-FK + `ON UPDATE CASCADE` + CHECK interaction behaves differently on the
123: target CockroachDB version than the invariants state (verify this early — it was proven on
124: PostgreSQL), then **stop and report** with the exact file and observed-vs-expected behaviour.
125: Reporting a blocker is the correct outcome. Silently redesigning around it is the only wrong one.

(End of file - total 125 lines)
</content>
```
