<content>
1: # IMPLEMENTATION_CONTRACT.md
2: 
3: **Status:** FROZEN. This is a contract between architect and implementer, not a design doc.
4: **Inputs the implementer is given:** this file + `db/001_schema.sql` (the approved DDL).
5: **Authority:** Where this file and any other document disagree, this file wins. Where this
6: file and `db/001_schema.sql` disagree, STOP and report (see §9). Do not redesign anything.
7: 
8: ---
9: 
10: ## 1. Project Objective
11: 
12: Build a transactional belief ledger for autonomous agents on CockroachDB. Claims are
13: first-class database rows carrying evidence, typed provenance, unpaid epistemic obligations
14: ("debt"), dependents, and promotion state. A claim becomes actionable only when its debt is
15: retired — a gate enforced by schema constraints and a composite foreign key, never by prompt.
16: When new evidence falsifies a claim, every dependent decision is un-promoted in one atomic
17: transaction. The MVP demonstrates this end-to-end over one real evidence feed, with two thin
18: agents and one dependency-graph view, and proves that serializable isolation is what prevents
19: concurrent agents from acting on a belief that has already been retracted.
20: 
21: ---
22: 
23: ## 2. Frozen Architecture
24: 
25: - **Three tables (+ one edge table, already approved):** `belief`, `belief_edge`, `evidence`,
26:   `action_intent`. No additional tables in the MVP. (The edge table was authorized by the
27:   gatekeeper as a correctness requirement, not a scope expansion.)
28: - **Two agents:** (a) **Claim Agent** — ingests evidence, extracts/types claims, retires debt,
29:   merges duplicates via vector similarity before insert; (b) **Security Agent** — reads promoted
30:   beliefs and writes action intents. Nothing else is an agent. The evidence ingestor is a
31:   deterministic pipe, folded into the Claim Agent, and does not count as a third agent.
32: - **One evidence feed:** a single external feed supplies real, timestamped, publicly verifiable
33:   evidence. The kernel is feed-agnostic; the feed name appears only in the ingestor and demo,
34:   never in the kernel package.
35: - **One graph:** a single dependency-graph view (Evidence → Belief → Action), nodes colored by
36:   status, with live transitions during the cascade. No second visualization.
37: 
38: ---
39: 
40: ## 3. Frozen Schema
41: 
42: The schema is `db/001_schema.sql` as approved. Do not modify it. It defines exactly four
43: tables and the following load-bearing constraints, which the kernel relies on and must not
44: duplicate in application code:
45: 
46: - `belief.claim_type` ∈ {`derived`,`accommodated`,`postulated`}; `belief.status` ∈
47:   {`entered`,`promoted`,`retracted`}; `belief.debt` is `TEXT[]`.
48: - `belief` CHECK `promoted_is_debt_free`: a row with `status='promoted'` must have empty `debt`
49:   and `final_truth=false`.
50: - `belief` UNIQUE `(id, status)` — the composite FK target.
51: - `action_intent` FK `(belief_id, belief_status) REFERENCES belief(id, status) ON UPDATE CASCADE`,
52:   plus CHECK `live_requires_promoted`: a row with `state='live'` must have `belief_status='promoted'`.
53: - `evidence.provenance_class` ∈ {`external_feed`,`reproducible_artifact`,`live_scan`,
54:   `operator_asserted`}; `content_sha256` NOT NULL.
55: - `belief.embedding` is nullable; the ledger is fully functional with every embedding NULL.
56: 
57: If any statement below cannot be implemented against this schema without changing it, STOP (§9).
58: 
59: ---
60: 
61: ## 4. Kernel API to Implement
62: 
63: Package `internal/kernel`. Signatures and behavioral contracts only. Every write path MUST run
64: inside `crdb.ExecuteTx` (serialization-failure retry). Error classification MUST use pgx
65: SQLSTATE codes, never substring matching on error text.
66: 
67: - `New(db *sql.DB) *Store`
68:   Wraps an open pool pointed at a database with `db/001_schema.sql` applied.
69: 
70: - `EnterBelief(ctx, scenarioID, claim string, ct ClaimType) (id string, err error)`
71:   Inserts a belief at the door: `status='entered'`, `debt` = full starting debt, unpromoted.
72:   Never gated. Returns the new id.
73: 
74: - `AddEvidence(ctx, scenarioID, beliefID, provenanceClass, sourceURL, contentSHA256 string) err`
75:   Inserts one evidence row for a belief. `contentSHA256` is required. Does not change belief state.
76: 
77: - `RetireDebt(ctx, beliefID, item string) err`
78:   Removes one debt item from `belief.debt`. Idempotent if the item is absent.
79: 
80: - `Promote(ctx, beliefID string) err`
81:   Sets `status='promoted'`. If the schema CHECK rejects it (open debt or final-truth language),
82:   return `ErrPromotionBlocked`. The gate is the database's, not the function's.
83: 
84: - `IntentOnPromoted(ctx, scenarioID, beliefID, action string) err`
85:   Inserts a live `action_intent`. If the composite FK rejects it (belief not currently promoted),
86:   return `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`.
87: 
88: - `RetractCascade(ctx, scenarioID, rootID string) (retracted int, err error)`
89:   In ONE transaction: (1) compute the transitive descendant set of `rootID` over `belief_edge`;
90:   (2) set all `live` intents on those beliefs to `cancelled`; (3) set those beliefs' `status` from
91:   `promoted` to `retracted`. Step 2 MUST precede step 3. Returns the number of beliefs retracted.
92: 
93: - `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted(ctx, scenarioID string) (int, error)`
94:   Returns the count of live intents whose belief is not promoted. MUST always return 0 in
95:   committed state (this is Invariant I-5 expressed as a query).
96: 
97: Exported sentinels required: `ErrPromotionBlocked`, `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`.
98: Exported value required: full starting debt list (the six debt items).
99: 
100: ---
101: 
102: ## 5. Invariants That Must Never Be Violated
103: 
104: - **I-1** No belief with `status='promoted'` has non-empty `debt`. (Schema CHECK.)
105: - **I-2** No belief with `status='promoted'` has `final_truth=true`. (Schema CHECK.)
106: - **I-3** No `live` action_intent references a belief that is not currently `promoted`.
107:   (Composite FK + `live_requires_promoted` CHECK.)
108: - **I-4** A belief carrying a live intent cannot transition to `retracted` without that intent
109:   first being cancelled. (Enforced by ON UPDATE CASCADE propagating status into the intent row,
110:   which then violates `live_requires_promoted` unless already cancelled.)
111: - **I-5** `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted` returns 0 in every committed state.
112: - **I-6** All invariant tests pass with every `embedding` column NULL. Vectors are an
113:   optimization, never part of belief semantics.
114: - **I-7** Every kernel write goes through `crdb.ExecuteTx`. No raw `db.Exec`/`db.Query` writes.
115: - **I-8** `RetractCascade` is a single transaction. Cancel-before-retract ordering is mandatory.
116: 
117: Any change that would require weakening or removing any of I-1..I-8 is out of scope: STOP (§9).
118: 
119: ---
120: 
121: ## 6. Ordered Implementation Plan (smallest compilable milestones)
122: 
123: - **M0 — Cluster + schema up.** Apply `db/001_schema.sql` to a CockroachDB cluster. Confirm
124:   cluster version, that `READ COMMITTED` is available, and that the composite FK + CHECK accept
125:   a promoted row and reject a debted one. No Go yet beyond a connection smoke test.
126: - **M1 — Kernel package compiles.** Implement all of §4 against the schema. `go build ./...` and
127:   `go vet ./...` clean. No agents, no UI.
128: - **M2 — Invariant test suite green.** Table-driven tests exercising I-1 through I-8 against a
129:   live cluster, including the single-transaction cascade and the NULL-embedding pass (I-6).
130: - **M3 — Isolation experiment.** A harness running the cascade-vs-new-intent race in three
131:   configurations: (1) naive schema @ READ COMMITTED → silent corruption committed
132:   (audit ≠ 0); (2) naive schema @ SERIALIZABLE → one txn refused with 40001, audit = 0;
133:   (3) hardened schema (the real `action_intent`) @ READ COMMITTED → FK refusal (23503), audit = 0.
134:   "Naive" means a comparison intent table without the composite FK; both transactions run correct
135:   application logic. This milestone is committed to CI.
136: - **M4 — Evidence ingestor + Claim Agent.** Pull the one real feed, hash and store snapshots,
137:   enter/type claims, retire debt, and merge duplicate claims via vector similarity before insert.
138: - **M5 — Security Agent + gate demo path.** Agent reads promoted beliefs and writes intents;
139:   provide the button-pushable path that attempts an action on an unpromoted belief and surfaces
140:   the raw constraint error.
141: - **M6 — Dependency-graph view.** One graph, status colors, live cascade transitions.
142: - **M7 — Demo acceptance green.** The mechanical acceptance checks (see §7 M7) pass against a
143:   fresh scenario.
144: 
145: Milestones are strictly ordered. Do not start Mn+1 before Mn meets its acceptance criteria.
146: 
147: ---
148: 
149: ## 7. Acceptance Criteria Per Milestone
150: 
151: - **M0:** Schema applies with zero errors; a promoted-with-debt UPDATE fails with SQLSTATE 23514;
152:   a promoted-debt-free UPDATE succeeds; `SHOW`/settings confirm READ COMMITTED availability.
153: - **M1:** `go build ./...` and `go vet ./...` exit 0. Every §4 function exists with the stated
154:   signature. Grep confirms zero raw write calls outside `crdb.ExecuteTx` (I-7).
155: - **M2:** All invariant tests pass. Specifically: promote-with-debt → `ErrPromotionBlocked`;
156:   intent-on-unpromoted → `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`; retract-with-live-intent (skipping cancel) is
157:   refused; full cascade cancels intents and retracts descendants; `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted` = 0;
158:   the entire suite passes a second time with all embeddings NULL.
159: - **M3:** Three cells produce, verbatim: cell 1 `audit ≠ 0`; cell 2 `ERROR 40001` and `audit = 0`;
160:   cell 3 `ERROR 23503` and `audit = 0`. Harness runs in CI and is deterministic.
161: - **M4:** Every `external_feed` evidence row has a resolving `source_url` and a matching
162:   `content_sha256`; a CI check walks all such rows and passes. A duplicate claim is merged, not
163:   forked (verified by count). No fabricated CVE IDs, advisories, or URLs.
164: - **M5:** On camera / on demand, an action attempt against an unpromoted belief returns the raw
165:   FK error (23503) with no LLM narration substituted for it.
166: - **M6:** Ingesting the falsifying evidence drives a visible cascade: dependent nodes change
167:   status and at least one live intent becomes cancelled, in the single-transaction path.
168: - **M7:** `demo_accept.sh` (mechanical subset) is green: run reaches the close; three distinct
169:   correct receipts; no internal vocabulary (`accommodated`/`postulated`/`derived`) in narrated
170:   output; every cell carries a receipt; cold-open number-free. Timing gates are human-verified at
171:   recording, not asserted by CI.
172: 
173: ---
174: 
175: ## 8. Explicit Non-Goals (deferred until after the hackathon)
176: 
177: Do not build, and do not spend time on: any table beyond the four; a third agent; a second
178: evidence feed; the full NVD embedding corpus (a small subset only); a live AWS estate or scanner
179: integration; SBOM/artifact ingestion beyond the single feed; OpenVEX export; multi-region
180: deployment (screenshots only, no build-out); stored confidence scores (confidence is derived
181: display, never a column); a fourth belief status (`promotable` is derived, not stored); any Lean
182: formalization; integration into the bchat host (the kernel stands alone; bchat integration was
183: rejected); agent sophistication (agents stay thin); authentication/RBAC beyond what the demo
184: session needs; a second visualization; a settings/config UI. Anything not required by §6 is out
185: of scope for the MVP.
186: 
187: ---
188: 
189: ## 9. Escalation Policy
190: 
191: If implementation reveals any of the following, STOP and report to the architect in a short note
192: naming the milestone, the exact statement or file, and the observed vs. expected behavior — do
193: NOT redesign, do NOT weaken an invariant, do NOT add a table/agent/feed to work around it:
194: 
195: - `db/001_schema.sql` cannot support a §4 behavior or a §5 invariant as written.
196: - Any invariant I-1..I-8 would have to be relaxed to make a milestone pass.
197: - The composite FK + `ON UPDATE CASCADE` + CHECK interaction behaves differently on the target
198:   CockroachDB version than §5 states (this is the one interaction most likely to differ from the
199:   Postgres proof; verify at M0 and report before proceeding).
200: - A milestone's acceptance criterion cannot be met without violating a non-goal in §8.
201: - Any ambiguity in this contract that has more than one reasonable implementation with different
202:   observable behavior.
203: 
204: Reporting the blocker is the correct outcome, exactly as a legitimate blocking finding — not a
205: failure. Silence-and-workaround is the only prohibited response.

<content>
1: # AGENTS.md
2: 
3: Think like a distributed systems engineer, not an LLM engineer.
4: 
5: ## Project
6: 
7: **Solvent** is a transactional belief ledger for autonomous agents.
8: 
9: The primary objective is **not** to build another AI agent or chatbot.
10: 
11: The objective is to build a **database-enforced memory system** where autonomous agents cannot act on beliefs that are no longer true.
12: 
13: The demo is only a presentation of this capability.
14: 
15: ---
16: 
17: # Core Thesis
18: 
19: Traditional agent memory stores facts.
20: 
21: Solvent stores **beliefs**.
22: 
23: Beliefs:
24: 
25: - carry evidence,
26: - evolve as new evidence arrives,
27: - may be promoted or retracted,
28: - govern whether an agent may act.
29: 
30: The database—not the LLM—determines whether an action is allowed.
31: 
32: ---
33: 
34: # Problem Statement
35: 
36: Modern AI agents often continue acting on stale information.
37: 
38: Prompt engineering cannot reliably prevent this.
39: 
40: Application code alone cannot reliably enforce correctness under concurrency.
41: 
42: Solvent applies distributed systems principles to agent memory.
43: 
44: Beliefs become transactional objects.
45: 
46: Actions become database-gated.
47: 
48: ---
49: 
50: # Primary Design Principle
51: 
52: > Memory is not remembering.
53: >
54: > Memory is refusing to act on what is no longer true.
55: 
56: This principle takes precedence over convenience or agent autonomy.
57: 
58: ---
59: 
60: # Architectural Principles
61: 
62: The architecture is frozen.
63: 
64: Do not redesign.
65: 
66: Current MVP consists of only:
67: 
68: - belief
69: - evidence
70: - action_intent
71: 
72: No additional tables unless explicitly approved.
73: 
74: The kernel remains domain-agnostic.
75: 
76: ---
77: 
78: # Implementation Status
79: 
80: Waves 1–4 are frozen. Each wave was implemented, reworked, adversarially reviewed, and verified against a live CockroachDB v26.2.0 cluster.
81: 
82: | Wave | Scope | Status | Tests |
83: |---|---|---|---|
84: | 1 — Normalize | `internal/normalize` | Frozen | 11 |
85: | 2 — Derive | `internal/derive` | Frozen | 16 |
86: | 3 — Kernel | `internal/belief`, `internal/intent`, kernel additions | Frozen | 48 |
87: | 4 — Pipeline | `internal/pipeline`, `cmd/solvent` | Frozen | 9 |
88: 
89: **Total: 84 tests, all passing.**
90: 
91: Frozen waves must not be modified. New work requires Technical Lead approval.
92: 
93: ---
94: 
95: # Evidence
96: 
97: The kernel never depends on a specific domain.
98: 
99: Cybersecurity, etcd, GitHub, package advisories, logistics, etc. are merely **EvidenceFeed** implementations.
100: 
101: The current MVP uses a real etcd issue history as demonstration data, processed through the normalize→derive→pipeline chain.
102: 
103: Changing the evidence source must not require kernel changes.
104: 
105: ---
106: 
107: # Agent Responsibilities
108: 
109: Agents do not decide truth.
110: 
111: Agents:
112: 
113: - observe evidence,
114: - propose beliefs,
115: - propose actions.
116: 
117: The Transactional Belief Ledger determines whether those actions remain valid.
118: 
119: ---
120: 
121: # Database Responsibilities
122: 
123: CockroachDB is not passive storage.
124: 
125: CockroachDB enforces:
126: 
127: - transactional consistency,
128: - promotion rules,
129: - action gating,
130: - atomic retraction,
131: - auditability.
132: 
133: Whenever possible, correctness belongs in schema and transactions rather than application code.
134: 
135: ---
136: 
137: # Engineering Philosophy
138: 
139: Prefer:
140: 
141: - explicit invariants
142: - receipts
143: - deterministic behavior
144: - transactional correctness
145: - minimal architecture
146: 
147: Avoid:
148: 
149: - hidden state
150: - prompt-only guarantees
151: - duplicated truth
152: - speculative abstractions
153: - unnecessary orchestration
154: 
155: ---
156: 
157: # Scope
158: 
159: This repository builds the **Transactional Belief Ledger**.
160: 
161: The demonstration application exists solely to make the ledger understandable.
162: 
163: If a design decision benefits the demo but weakens the ledger, reject it.
164: 
165: If a design decision strengthens the ledger but simplifies the demo, prefer the ledger.
166: 
167: The ledger is the product.
168: 
169: The demo is the proof.
170: 
171: ---
172: 
173: # Development Rules
174: 
175: - Preserve the frozen architecture.
176: - Frozen waves must not be modified. New work requires Technical Lead approval.
177: - Do not silently redesign behavior.
178: - Unknowns become receipts.
179: - Implementation follows approved specifications.
180: - Database correctness takes precedence over application convenience.
181: 
182: When in doubt, ask:
183: 
184: > "Should this responsibility belong to the database instead?"

(End of file - total 184 lines)
</content>

<path>/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/README.md</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: # README.md
2: 
3: Standing context for AI agents working in this repo. Read this before editing code.
4: This is orientation, not a task list — the milestone plan lives in `IMPLEMENTATION_CONTRACT.md`.
5: 
6: ## What this is (primary)
7: 
8: This repository is a **transactional belief ledger for autonomous agents**. It is not a
9: chatbot, a RAG store, or a security tool. Its job is to hold *claims* as first-class
10: database rows that carry their evidence, their typed provenance, their unpaid epistemic
11: obligations ("debt"), their dependents, and their promotion state — and to make a claim
12: **actionable only when it has earned it**, with that gate enforced by the database schema
13: rather than by any prompt. When new evidence falsifies a claim, every decision downstream
14: of it is un-promoted in one atomic transaction. Concurrent agents revising the same belief
15: is a write-skew problem; serializable isolation is why the substrate is CockroachDB.
16: 
17: The mental model, in five lines (all load-bearing):
18: - **Ideas enter free.** A belief is written cheaply, fully in debt, `status='entered'`.
19: - **Promotion costs debt.** A belief becomes actionable only when its debt is empty.
20: - **New evidence creates new debt.** Falsification is debt reappearing, not deletion.
21: - **Debt does not kill.** Retracted beliefs are preserved — this is the audit story.
22: - **Knowledge oscillates, it does not accumulate.** Memory here is *subtractive*.
23: 
24: One sentence for humans: *beliefs enter free; actions cost promotion; the gate is in the tables.*
25: 
26: ## Invariants — never violate these
27: 
28: - **I-1** No `promoted` belief has non-empty `debt`. (schema CHECK `promoted_is_debt_free`)
29: - **I-2** No `promoted` belief has `final_truth = true`. (same CHECK)
30: - **I-3** No `live` action_intent references a non-`promoted` belief. (composite FK + CHECK)
31: - **I-4** A belief with a live intent cannot become `retracted` until that intent is cancelled.
32:   (composite FK `ON UPDATE CASCADE` propagates status into the intent, then the CHECK fires)
33: - **I-5** `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted` returns 0 in every committed state.
34: - **I-6** Every invariant holds with all `embedding` columns NULL. Vectors are an optimization,
35:   never part of belief semantics.
36: - **I-7** Every kernel write goes through `crdb.ExecuteTx`. No raw `db.Exec`/`db.Query` writes.
37: - **I-8** `RetractCascade` is a single transaction; cancel-before-retract ordering is mandatory.
38: 
39: If a change would require weakening any of I-1..I-8, it is out of scope — stop and report.
40: 
41: ## Enforcement locus — the thing agents get wrong
42: 
43: **I-1 through I-4 are enforced by the database, not by Go.** They live in CHECK constraints,
44: a composite `UNIQUE (id, status)` target, and a `FOREIGN KEY (belief_id, belief_status) …
45: ON UPDATE CASCADE`. Do **not** re-implement these as application-level guards in Go. Duplicated
46: guards drift from the schema, contradict it under concurrency, and give a false sense of safety.
47: The kernel's job is to *route writes through transactions* and *translate the database's refusal
48: into a typed Go error* — not to pre-check what the schema already refuses. The whole thesis is
49: that the gate is structural; keep it structural.
50: 
51: ## Architecture at a glance
52: 
53: - **Schema (frozen):** four tables in `db/001_schema.sql` — `belief`, `belief_edge`,
54:   `evidence`, `action_intent`. Do not modify this file (see boundaries).
55:   - `claim_type` ∈ {`derived`,`accommodated`,`postulated`}; `status` ∈
56:     {`entered`,`promoted`,`retracted`} — there is no fourth status. `debt` is `TEXT[]`.
57:   - `evidence.provenance_class` ∈ {`external_feed`,`reproducible_artifact`,`live_scan`,
58:     `operator_asserted`}; `content_sha256` is required.
59: - **Kernel:** `internal/kernel` (package `kernel`). Public surface, behaviour only:
60:   `New`, `EnterBelief`, `AddEvidence`, `RetireDebt`, `Promote` (→ `ErrPromotionBlocked`),
61:   `IntentOnPromoted` (→ `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`), `RetractCascade`, `AuditLiveOnNonPromoted`,
62:   plus `FullDebt` and the `ClaimType` constants. This package must not import demo/domain code.
63: - **Pipeline:** `internal/pipeline` chains normalize → derive → belief.Process → ProposeIfNew
64:   into a single pass over evidence fixtures. Evidence aggregation is CVE-aware: each distinct
65:   CVE-bearing claim remains independent; non-CVE supporting evidence merges into the matching
66:   primary. Contradictions emit `slog.Warn` with source provenance and produce no ledger mutation.
67:   Intent proposal is replay-idempotent under sequential execution.
68: - **CLI:** `cmd/solvent` is the demo entry point. It runs the full pipeline against local JSON
69:   fixtures and prints a deterministic transcript with source provenance, belief state, and audit.
70: - **Substrate:** CockroachDB. `cockroach-go/v2` (`crdb.ExecuteTx`).
71: - **Agents (thin):** a *claim agent* (ingest evidence → enter/type claims → retire debt →
72:   merge duplicates by vector similarity before insert) and a *security agent* (read promoted
73:   beliefs → write intents). Keep them dumb; the memory layer is the intelligence. The pipeline
74:   serves as the integration layer for the MVP demo.
75: 
76: ## Go + CockroachDB conventions
77: 
78: - **All writes via `crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, db, nil, func(tx) error { … })`.** It runs the
79:   40001 retry/savepoint protocol. A write outside it is a bug (I-7).
80: - **Classify errors by SQLSTATE, never by string.** Use `errors.As` to reach the pgx
81:   `SQLState()`; `23514` = CHECK violation (→ `ErrPromotionBlocked`), `23503` = FK violation
82:   (→ `ErrActionOnUnpromoted`), `40001` = serialization failure (let `ExecuteTx` retry).
83: - **Serializable is the default on CockroachDB, and that default is the point.** Do not reach
84:   for `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` as the primary safety mechanism — explicit locks are per-call-site
85:   vigilance that every future edit must remember; serializable requires remembering nothing.
86:   `READ COMMITTED` appears **only** in the isolation experiment, deliberately, to contrast.
87: - **Confidence is derived, never stored.** There is no confidence column and must never be one.
88:   If a UI needs a legibility signal, derive it (e.g. count of open debt); never persist a scalar.
89: - **The cascade is one transaction:** recursive walk of `belief_edge` from the root, cancel live
90:   intents on all descendants, then un-promote them — in that order, in a single `ExecuteTx`.
91: - **Vector column is nullable and used for merge-not-fork** (dedup on write), not retrieval.
92:   The ledger must be fully correct with it NULL (I-6).
93: 
94: ## Hard boundaries (do not cross without escalation)
95: 
96: - **The schema is frozen.** Do not edit `db/001_schema.sql`. A needed schema change is a
97:   blocking finding to report, not an edit to make.
98: - **Frozen waves must not be modified.** Waves 1–4 (normalize, derive, belief/intent/kernel,
99:   pipeline/CLI) are frozen. New work requires Technical Lead approval.
100: - **No new tables, agents, or evidence feeds.** The system is deliberately three-tables-two-
101:   agents-one-feed-one-graph. Additions belong in a post-hackathon backlog.
102: - **Kernel stays domain-agnostic.** No feed name (KEV, GitHub, etcd, …) appears in
103:   `internal/kernel`. Domain lives in the ingestor and the demo only.
104: - **No fabricated evidence — ever.** No invented IDs, advisories, or URLs. `external_feed`
105:   rows must have a resolving `source_url` and a matching `content_sha256`; CI enforces this.
106:   In a system whose entire pitch is provenance, fabricated evidence is self-refuting.
107: - **No internal vocabulary on user-facing surfaces.** Words like `accommodated`/`postulated`/
108:   `derived` are schema values, not narration; they stay out of the demo and any UI copy.
109: 
110: ## The demo (secondary)
111: 
112: The demo is **Layer 4 and swappable** — it exists to make the kernel legible, and the kernel
113: must never depend on it. The current domain candidate is a live software-project belief stream
114: (GitHub-issue timelines; etcd is the leading choice for its dated, public "declared stable →
115: publicly retracted" oscillation). Whatever the domain, the demo must prove exactly three things,
116: all backed by real receipts: the **gate** (an action on an unpromoted belief is refused by the
117: FK, raw error shown), the **cascade** (new evidence falsifies a belief and downstream intents
118: cancel atomically), and the **three-cell isolation experiment** (naive schema @ READ COMMITTED →
119: silent corruption; @ SERIALIZABLE → 40001 refusal; hardened schema @ READ COMMITTED → FK refusal).
120: Swapping the domain changes the ingestor and the narrative; it changes nothing above Layer 4.
121: 
122: The CLI entry point is `cmd/solvent`. Run it with `--scenario <UUID> --reset` to process the
123: full etcd KEV fixture set and produce a deterministic transcript proving pipeline correctness.
124: 
125: ## Current State
126: 
127: Waves 1–4 are frozen. Each wave was implemented, reworked, adversarially reviewed, and verified
128: against a live CockroachDB v26.2.0 cluster.
129: 
130: | Wave | Scope | Status | Tests |
131: |---|---|---|---|
132: | 1 — Normalize | `internal/normalize` | Frozen | 11 |
133: | 2 — Derive | `internal/derive` | Frozen | 16 |
134: | 3 — Kernel | `internal/belief`, `internal/intent`, kernel additions | Frozen | 48 |
135: | 4 — Pipeline | `internal/pipeline`, `cmd/solvent` | Frozen | 9 |
136: 
137: **Total: 84 tests, all passing.** Serial execution required (test harness shares a single
138: CockroachDB database).
139: 
140: ## Before you commit
141: 
142: - `go build ./...` and `go vet ./...` clean; no write path outside `crdb.ExecuteTx`.
143: - All 84 tests pass (run serially): `go test ./internal/kernel/ ./internal/belief/ ./internal/intent/ ./internal/derive/ ./internal/normalize/ ./internal/pipeline/ -count=1`.
144: - Kernel invariant tests green — and green a second time with all embeddings NULL (I-6).
145: - The pure-SQL proof still passes: `proof/02_lifecycle_and_invariants.sql` and the isolation
146:   harness in `proof/`. These are the source of truth the Go must reproduce, not diverge from.
147: 
148: ## Escalation
149: 
150: If the schema cannot support a required behaviour, if an invariant would have to be relaxed,
151: or if the composite-FK + `ON UPDATE CASCADE` + CHECK interaction behaves differently on the
152: target CockroachDB version than the invariants state (verify this early — it was proven on
153: PostgreSQL), then **stop and report** with the exact file and observed-vs-expected behaviour.
154: Reporting a blocker is the correct outcome. Silently redesigning around it is the only wrong one.

(End of file - total 154 lines)
</content>
```

1: -- The gatekeeper's mandated sequence, then the invariants as executable refusals.
2: -- Convention: forbidden operations are attempted inside DO blocks; the kernel PASSES
3: -- a test by REFUSING the operation. If the operation is allowed, we raise FAIL loudly.
4: 
5: \set ON_ERROR_STOP on
6: \set QUIET on
7: \pset tuples_only on
8: 
9: -- ---------- fixtures ----------
10: INSERT INTO belief (id, scenario_id, claim, claim_type) VALUES
11:  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a','11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
12:   'root: exposure signal absent for asset A','accommodated'),
13:  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b','11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
14:   'child: asset A triage may be deferred','derived');
15: INSERT INTO belief_edge (parent_id, child_id) VALUES
16:  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a','00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b');
17: INSERT INTO evidence (scenario_id, belief_id, provenance_class, source_url, content_sha256)
18:  VALUES ('11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111','00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a',
19:          'external_feed','https://example.invalid/feed/item','deadbeef');
20: 
21: -- ---------- I-1: promotion with open debt is unrepresentable ----------
22: DO $$ BEGIN
23:   UPDATE belief SET status='promoted' WHERE id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a';
24:   RAISE EXCEPTION 'I-1 FAIL: promoted with open debt';
25: EXCEPTION WHEN check_violation THEN RAISE NOTICE 'I-1 PASS: % ', SQLERRM; END $$;
26: 
27: -- retire all debt on both beliefs (the Test step of the pipeline)
28: UPDATE belief SET debt='{}' WHERE scenario_id='11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';
29: 
30: -- ---------- I-2: final-truth language blocks promotion ----------
31: UPDATE belief SET final_truth=true WHERE id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a';
32: DO $$ BEGIN
33:   UPDATE belief SET status='promoted' WHERE id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a';
34:   RAISE EXCEPTION 'I-2 FAIL: promoted final-truth claim';
35: EXCEPTION WHEN check_violation THEN RAISE NOTICE 'I-2 PASS: %', SQLERRM; END $$;
36: UPDATE belief SET final_truth=false WHERE id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a';
37: 
38: -- ---------- I-3: intent on an unpromoted belief is refused by the FK ----------
39: DO $$ BEGIN
40:   INSERT INTO action_intent (scenario_id, belief_id, action)
41:   VALUES ('11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111','00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b','defer');
42:   RAISE EXCEPTION 'I-3 FAIL: intent accepted on unpromoted belief';
43: EXCEPTION WHEN foreign_key_violation THEN RAISE NOTICE 'I-3 PASS: %', SQLERRM; END $$;
44: 
45: -- ---------- promote (the throne, debt paid) ----------
46: UPDATE belief SET status='promoted'
47:  WHERE id IN ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a','00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b');
48: 
49: -- intent now succeeds
50: INSERT INTO action_intent (id, scenario_id, belief_id, action) VALUES
51:  ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1','11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
52:   '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b','defer_patch');
53: 
54: -- ---------- I-4: wrong-order retraction (skip cancel) is refused by the schema ----------
55: DO $$ BEGIN
56:   UPDATE belief SET status='retracted' WHERE id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b';
57:   RAISE EXCEPTION 'I-4 FAIL: retracted a belief carrying a live intent';
58: EXCEPTION WHEN check_violation THEN RAISE NOTICE 'I-4 PASS: %', SQLERRM; END $$;
59: 
60: -- ---------- the correct cascade: cancel-then-retract, one transaction ----------
61: BEGIN;
62: CREATE TEMP TABLE _desc ON COMMIT DROP AS
63:   WITH RECURSIVE d(id) AS (
64:     SELECT '00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a'::uuid
65:     UNION
66:     SELECT e.child_id FROM belief_edge e JOIN d ON e.parent_id = d.id
67:   ) SELECT id FROM d;
68: UPDATE action_intent SET state='cancelled'
69:   WHERE state='live' AND belief_id IN (SELECT id FROM _desc);
70: UPDATE belief SET status='retracted'
71:   WHERE status='promoted' AND id IN (SELECT id FROM _desc);
72: COMMIT;
73: 
74: -- ---------- post-cascade assertions ----------
75: SELECT CASE WHEN count(*)=2 THEN 'CASCADE PASS: both beliefs retracted'
76:        ELSE 'CASCADE FAIL' END
77: FROM belief WHERE status='retracted'
78:   AND scenario_id='11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111';
79: 
80: -- Bug-B positive check: the cancelled intent SURVIVES, with belief_status
81: -- auto-cascaded to 'retracted' — history preserved, invariant intact.
82: SELECT CASE WHEN state='cancelled' AND belief_status='retracted'
83:        THEN 'BUG-B FIX PASS: cancelled intent carries cascaded status'
84:        ELSE 'BUG-B FIX FAIL: '||state||'/'||belief_status END
85: FROM action_intent WHERE id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1';
86: 
87: -- ---------- I-5: global audit — zero live intents on non-promoted beliefs ----------
88: SELECT CASE WHEN count(*)=0 THEN 'I-5 PASS: audit clean'
89:        ELSE 'I-5 FAIL: '||count(*)||' corrupt rows' END
90: FROM action_intent a JOIN belief b ON b.id=a.belief_id
91: WHERE a.state='live' AND b.status <> 'promoted';
92: 
93: -- I-6 is satisfied by construction: \d belief contains no vector column.

## 1. Schema Tables (`db/001_schema.sql`)

## 2. Retraction

### 2a. RetractCascade in `internal/kernel/kernel.go`

**File:** `/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/internal/kernel/kernel.go`, lines 111-147

**YES, it is implemented.** The function signature is:

```go
func (s *Store) RetractCascade(ctx context.Context, scenarioID, rootID string) (int, error)
```

It executes two statements in one `crdb.ExecuteTx` transaction:
1. `sqlRetractCascadeCancel` (line 128) -- cancels live intents on the descendant set
2. `sqlRetractCascadeRetract` (line 132) -- sets belief status to 'retracted'

The SQL implementations are in `internal/kernel/sql.go`, lines 80-92. They use a recursive CTE (`WITH RECURSIVE d(id)`) defined at lines 66-75 that walks the `belief_edge` graph.

It is also declared in the `Contract` interface at `/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/internal/kernel/contract.go`, line 22:
```go
RetractCascade(context.Context, string, string) (int, error)
```

### 2b. Contradiction handling in `internal/belief/belief.go`

**File:** `/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/internal/belief/belief.go`, lines 38-48

**Contradictions do NOT trigger RetractCascade.** The code explicitly says "automatic retraction deferred":

```go
// Contradictions: retraction path.
if len(b.Contradicts) > 0 {
    for _, c := range b.Contradicts {
        slog.Warn("belief.Process: contradiction received, automatic retraction deferred",
            "source_url", c.SourceURL,
            "source_type", c.SourceType,
            "claim", b.Claim,
        )
    }
    return nil
}
```

The function returns `nil` without making any database writes. The comment on line 26 says `"Contradictions bypass steps 2-4 and call RetractCascade."` -- but the actual code on line 47 returns nil without calling RetractCascade. The comment is aspirational/incorrect relative to the implementation.

### 2c. Contradiction handling in `internal/pipeline/pipeline.go`

**File:** `/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/internal/pipeline/pipeline.go`

In `ProcessEvidence` (line 86): contradictions produce a `Result{Contradiction: true}` and emit an `slog.Warn`. No ledger mutation.

In `Run` (lines 361-379): contradictions collected in Phase 4 are logged with `slog.Warn` and appended to results. The comment at line 361 says: `"// Phase 4: record contradictions with warning (F1). No ledger mutation."`

**No beliefs are created, no evidence is added, no ledger is mutated for contradictions.** They are detected and logged only.

## 6. CLI (`cmd/solvent/main.go`)

**File:** `/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/cmd/solvent/main.go` (148 lines)

### Flags

| Flag | Default | Description |
|------|---------|-------------|
| `--dsn` | `testdb.DSN()` (from env `FABLE_DSN`) | CockroachDB connection string |
| `--schema` | `db/001_schema.sql` | Path to frozen DDL |
| `--scenario` | `""` (required) | Scenario ID for deterministic replay |
| `--reset` | `false` | Drop and recreate the target database first |
| `--out` | `""` (stdout) | Transcript output path |

### Required flags

Both `--dsn` and `--scenario` are required; the program exits with error if either is empty.

### What it outputs

A "SOLVENT TRANSCRIPT" with:
1. Scenario ID and result count
2. For each result: source, source_url, claim, classification, belief_id, promoted status, debt item count, intent state
3. Contradictions are marked with `contradiction: true` and `ledger_mutation: none`
4. An audit section: `audit_live_on_nonpromoted: <N>`
5. A final `VERDICT: PASS` or `VERDICT: FAIL`

### Fixture directory

Hardcoded to `internal/derive/testdata` (line 42). The fixture files are read from there.

### Exact command to run

```bash
go run ./cmd/solvent --dsn "postgresql://user:pass@host:26257/dbname?sslmode=disable" --scenario "test-scenario-1" --reset
```

Or with environment variable:
```bash
export FABLE_DSN="postgresql://user:pass@host:26257/dbname?sslmode=disable"
go run ./cmd/solvent --scenario "test-scenario-1" --reset
```

## 8. Contradiction Handling

### What happens when a contradiction is detected?

**At the pipeline level** (`internal/pipeline/pipeline.go`):

In `ProcessEvidence` (lines 86-97):
```go
if len(d.Contradicts) > 0 {
    r.Contradiction = true
    for _, c := range d.Contradicts {
        slog.Warn("pipeline: contradiction received, no ledger mutation",
            "source_url", c.SourceURL,
            "source_type", c.SourceType,
            "claim", d.Claim,
        )
    }
    results = append(results, r)
    continue
}
```

In `Run` (lines 361-379):
```go
// Phase 4: record contradictions with warning (F1). No ledger mutation.
for _, c := range contradictions {
    for _, ev := range c.Contradicts {
        slog.Warn("pipeline: contradiction received, no ledger mutation",
            "source_url", ev.SourceURL,
            "source_type", ev.SourceType,
            "claim", c.Claim,
        )
    }
    ...
    allResults = append(allResults, Result{
        Normalized:    contradictionNorm,
        Contradiction: true,
        Beliefs:       []derive.DerivedBelief{c},
    })
}
```

**At the belief level** (`internal/belief/belief.go`, lines 38-48):

```go
if len(b.Contradicts) > 0 {
    for _, c := range b.Contradicts {
        slog.Warn("belief.Process: contradiction received, automatic retraction deferred",
            "source_url", c.SourceURL,
            "source_type", c.SourceType,
            "claim", b.Claim,
        )
    }
    return nil
}
```

### Summary of contradiction behavior

| Question | Answer |
|----------|--------|
| Does it mutate the ledger? | **NO.** Both `belief.Process` and pipeline functions return without DB writes. |
| Does it create beliefs? | **NO.** `EnsureBelief` is never called for contradictions. |
| Does it add evidence? | **NO.** `AddEvidence` is never called for contradictions. |
| Does it trigger retraction? | **NO.** Despite the comment on line 26 of belief.go saying "Contradictions bypass steps 2-4 and call RetractCascade," the actual code returns nil without calling `RetractCascade`. The comment is aspirational/inaccurate. |
| What does it do? | Logs warnings via `slog.Warn` and records the contradiction in the `Result` struct with `Contradiction: true`. The transcript output shows `ledger_mutation: none`. |

### Documentation discrepancy

The comment at `/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/internal/belief/belief.go`, line 26 states:
```go
// Contradictions bypass steps 2-4 and call RetractCascade.
```

But the implementation at lines 38-48 does **not** call `RetractCascade`. It logs and returns nil. The AGENTS.md description of "automatic retraction deferred" (line 41) matches the actual behavior; the doc comment on line 26 does not.

1: // Package kernel is the transactional belief ledger.
2: //
3: // The invariants are the schema's, not this package's: db/001_schema.sql enforces
4: // I-1 through I-5 with CHECK constraints and a composite foreign key. This package's
5: // job is narrower and mechanical — route every write through crdb.ExecuteTx so
6: // serialization failures (40001) retry correctly, keep the cascade in ONE
7: // transaction with cancel strictly before retract (I-8), and name the database's
8: // refusals without ever substituting its own judgement for them.
9: //
10: // The package imports no driver. Errors are classified through
11: // interface{ SQLState() string }, which *pgconn.PgError satisfies; the driver is
12: // selected at the cmd/ edge.
13: package kernel
14: 
15: import (
16: 	"context"
17: 	"database/sql"
18: 
19: 	"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach-go/v2/crdb"
20: )
21: 
22: // FullDebt is the full starting debt every belief carries at the door.
23: //
24: // These six items are byte-identical, and in the same order, to the ARRAY[...]
25: // default on belief.debt in db/001_schema.sql. The duplication is deliberate — the
26: // Go constant is what EnterBelief writes — but it means the two can drift, so any
27: // change to one must change the other.
28: var FullDebt = []string{
29: 	"needMap", "needInvariant", "needToyCheck",
30: 	"needNullModel", "needObstruction", "needFaithfulnessReview",
31: }
32: 
33: // ClaimType is the typed provenance of a claim. The three values are exactly the
34: // schema's check_claim_type set.
35: type ClaimType string
36: 
37: const (
38: 	Derived      ClaimType = "derived"
39: 	Accommodated ClaimType = "accommodated"
40: 	Postulated   ClaimType = "postulated"
41: )
42: 
43: // Store wraps an open pool pointed at a database with db/001_schema.sql applied.
44: type Store struct{ db *sql.DB }
45: 
46: // New wraps db. It does not ping, migrate, or validate the schema.
47: func New(db *sql.DB) *Store { return &Store{db: db} }
48: 
49: // EnterBelief inserts a claim at the door: status 'entered', carrying the full
50: // starting debt, unpromoted. Ideas enter free — this path is never gated.
51: func (s *Store) EnterBelief(ctx context.Context, scenarioID, claim string, ct ClaimType) (string, error) {
52: 	// A copy, so a caller that mutates FullDebt cannot corrupt this write.
53: 	debt := append([]string(nil), FullDebt...)
54: 
55: 	var id string
56: 	err := crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
57: 		return tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, sqlEnterBelief,
58: 			scenarioID, claim, string(ct), debt,
59: 		).Scan(&id)
60: 	})
61: 	if err != nil {
62: 		return "", err
63: 	}
64: 	return id, nil
65: }
66: 
67: // AddEvidence records one evidence row for a belief. contentSHA256 is required —
68: // the column is NOT NULL and this function substitutes no placeholder for a hash it
69: // was not given. Belief state is unchanged.
70: func (s *Store) AddEvidence(ctx context.Context, scenarioID, beliefID, provenanceClass, sourceURL, contentSHA256 string) error {
71: 	return crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
72: 		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, sqlAddEvidence,
73: 			scenarioID, beliefID, provenanceClass, sourceURL, contentSHA256)
74: 		return err
75: 	})
76: }
77: 
78: // RetireDebt removes one debt item. It is idempotent: array_remove on an absent item
79: // changes nothing, and affecting zero rows is success, not an error.
80: func (s *Store) RetireDebt(ctx context.Context, beliefID, item string) error {
81: 	return crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
82: 		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, sqlRetireDebt, beliefID, item)
83: 		return err
84: 	})
85: }
86: 
87: // Promote moves a belief to promoted.
88: //
89: // The gate is the schema's promoted_is_debt_free CHECK, not this function's: no
90: // debt is inspected here. When the database refuses with 23514 the refusal is named
91: // ErrPromotionBlocked and the driver error is preserved underneath it.
92: func (s *Store) Promote(ctx context.Context, beliefID string) error {
93: 	return crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
94: 		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, sqlPromote, beliefID)
95: 		return wrapIf(sqlStateCheckViolation, ErrPromotionBlocked, err)
96: 	})
97: }
98: 
99: // IntentOnPromoted records intent to act on a belief.
100: //
101: // The composite FK (belief_id, 'promoted') -> belief(id, status) physically refuses
102: // this unless the belief is currently promoted (I-3). A 23503 refusal is named
103: // ErrActionOnUnpromoted with the driver error preserved underneath.
104: func (s *Store) IntentOnPromoted(ctx context.Context, scenarioID, beliefID, action string) error {
105: 	return crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
106: 		_, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, sqlIntentOnPromoted, scenarioID, beliefID, action)
107: 		return wrapIf(sqlStateFKViolation, ErrActionOnUnpromoted, err)
108: 	})
109: }
110: 
111: // RetractCascade un-promotes a belief and everything derived from it, in ONE
112: // transaction.
113: //
114: // Order is mandatory (I-8): live intents on the descendant set are cancelled FIRST,
115: // then the descendants are retracted. Reversing it does not corrupt anything — the
116: // composite FK's ON UPDATE CASCADE propagates the new status into each intent row and
117: // live_requires_promoted then refuses the write — but it does fail, which is the
118: // schema enforcing the ordering rather than this code remembering it.
119: //
120: // Scenario-scoped per D-032: traversal and both updates are confined to scenarioID.
121: //
122: // retracted is the RowsAffected of the belief update only. It counts belief rows
123: // retracted, never cancelled intents.
124: func (s *Store) RetractCascade(ctx context.Context, scenarioID, rootID string) (int, error) {
125: 	var retracted int
126: 	err := crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
127: 		// Cancel first.
128: 		if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, sqlRetractCascadeCancel, rootID, scenarioID); err != nil {
129: 			return err
130: 		}
131: 		// Then retract.
132: 		res, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx, sqlRetractCascadeRetract, rootID, scenarioID)
133: 		if err != nil {
134: 			return err
135: 		}
136: 		n, err := res.RowsAffected()
137: 		if err != nil {
138: 			return err
139: 		}
140: 		retracted = int(n)
141: 		return nil
142: 	})
143: 	if err != nil {
144: 		return 0, err
145: 	}
146: 	return retracted, nil
147: }
148: 
149: // AuditLiveOnNonPromoted is invariant I-5 expressed as a query: the count of live
150: // intents whose belief is not promoted, scoped to one scenario. It must be 0 in
151: // every committed state.
152: //
153: // This is the one read path, so it does not use crdb.ExecuteTx — I-7 governs writes.
154: func (s *Store) AuditLiveOnNonPromoted(ctx context.Context, scenarioID string) (int, error) {
155: 	var n int
156: 	err := s.db.QueryRowContext(ctx, sqlAuditLiveOnNonPromoted, scenarioID).Scan(&n)
157: 	return n, err
158: }
159: 
160: // EnsureBelief returns the ID of a belief with the given claim in the scenario.
161: // If no such belief exists, it creates one with the given claim type, full starting
162: // debt, and status='entered'.
163: //
164: // The find-or-create is a single transaction — no TOCTOU boundary.
165: // The caller does not need to know whether the belief was newly created.
166: func (s *Store) EnsureBelief(ctx context.Context, scenarioID, claim string, ct ClaimType) (string, error) {
167: 	var id string
168: 	err := crdb.ExecuteTx(ctx, s.db, nil, func(tx *sql.Tx) error {
169: 		return tx.QueryRowContext(ctx, sqlEnsureBelief,
170: 			scenarioID, claim, string(ct),
171: 		).Scan(&id)
172: 	})
173: 	if err != nil {
174: 		return "", err
175: 	}
176: 	return id, nil
177: }

(End of file - total 177 lines)
</content>
```

Frozen code (Wave 3), not fixed. Documented here.\n\n2. **Sequential test execution required.** The test harness shares a single CockroachDB database (`fable_test`). Running `go test ./...` in parallel causes race conditions. Serial execution is documented.\n\n3. **Intent proposal is replay-idempotent, not concurrent-safe.** `ProposeIfNew` uses SELECT-then-INSERT. Concurrent duplicate-intent prevention is deferred.\n\n---\n\n## 13. Final Recommendation\n\n**READY TO PUBLISH.**\n\nAll documentation claims match the actual repository state. The one discrepancy (missing `belief_edge` in AGENTS.md) has been fixed. The aspirational comment in `belief.go:26` is in frozen code and documented as a known limitation. A judge cloning this repository will find that the README describes what actually happens.\n\n---\n\n— End of public-repo audit —\n",
  "filePath": "/home/chaschel/Desktop/cockroach/hackathon/go/PUBLIC_REPO_AUDIT.md"
}
